We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [71] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出企业语言模型（CLM）框架，通过五个能力平面和四个架构支柱（神经符号网格、技能图、活体数字孪生、深度安全层），将企业结构化、非结构化、多模态及隐性知识转化为基于本体的企业基础，以解决企业AI部署因缺乏组织决策/谈判/执行的结构化基底而失败的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署失败并非源于模型能力不足，而是组织缺乏编码其如何决策、协商和执行的底层结构化基底。通用LLM不携带企业特有的本体先验；RAG脆弱且无法通往可执行行动；静态剧本编码了逻辑却无法推理或适应。这要求将隐性知识捕获、本体落地、主权部署与可审计执行从一开始就协同设计。

Method: CLM框架包含五个能力平面和四大架构支柱：神经符号网格（Neurosymbolic Mesh）将生成模型与知识图谱耦合；技能图（Skill Graph）对可复用战术、角色、异议与目标进行类型化与组合；活体数字孪生（Living Digital Twins）将功能领域建模为推理替身；深度安全层（Deep Security Layer）保障主权、可追溯性与人工监督。此外采用Spec-as-Code范式弥合落地意图与可执行工件之间的鸿沟。

Result: 论文提出四项贡献：将CLM定义为一个独立的研究对象；引入技能图实现刻意的可组合可解释性；提出"智慧倾听者"效应（Wisdom Listener），即具备隐性知识能力的基础会随使用而价值累积，连接动态能力与组织学习；并在巴西一家获JCI认证的三级医院提供了在LGPD下的六个成熟度阶段中三个阶段的实施证据。

Conclusion: CLM是这类以基础为中心架构的具体实例化。该架构从设计之初就将隐性知识捕获、本体支撑、主权部署与审计式执行协同整合，为企业级AI的成功部署提供了新的系统化路径。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployments fail not from model inadequacy, but because organizations lack a structured substrate encoding how they decide, negotiate, and execute. Generic LLMs carry no firm-specific ontological priors; RAG remains brittle, with no path to executable action; static playbooks encode logic but cannot reason or adapt. This demands an architecture treating tacit-knowledge capture, ontological grounding, sovereign deployment, and auditable actuation as co-designed from the start. This paper introduces the Corporate Language Model (CLM), a framework transforming a firm's structured, unstructured, multimodal, and tacit knowledge into an ontology-grounded enterprise foundation upon which reasoning and governed execution are composed. CLM has five capability planes and four architectural pillars: a Neurosymbolic Mesh coupling generative models with a knowledge graph; a Skill Graph where reusable tactics, personas, objections, and goals are typed and composed; Living Digital Twins modeling functional areas as reasoning surrogates; and a Deep Security Layer enforcing sovereignty, traceability, and human oversight. A Spec-as-Code paradigm bridges grounded intent and executable artifact. CLM is one instantiation of this foundation-centric class. Four contributions follow: CLM is defined as a distinct object of study; the Skill Graph is introduced for compositional explainability by construction; the Wisdom Listener effect is proposed, whereby tacit-capable foundations compound in value with use, connecting to dynamic capabilities and organizational learning; and evidence from a JCI-accredited tertiary hospital in Brazil instantiates three of the six maturity stages under LGPD.

</details>


### [72] [HarvestBench: Measuring Whether LLM Agents Will Pay to Avoid Killing Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04444)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Miles Tidmarsh,Matthias Endres,Anshuman Singh,Jeremiah Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarvestBench 是首个为"避免副作用"标价的基准测试，将副作用命名为有生命的动物。它是一个无记忆、无大模型评分的强化学习网格农场模拟，用 LLM 子代理驾驶两台拖拉机收割玉米；当动物挡路时，模型需在无燃料成本直行（杀死动物）或支付标价绕行之间抉择。九个模型在7,201次定价决策中，杀伤率从0.4%到98.8%不等，与模型能力无关；部分模型对价格敏感（弹性0.09–1.69）；道德briefing会显著降低伤害率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的"agent副作用"评测存在，但没有一个把避免副作用的"代价"量化，也没有将副作用明确命名为有生命的生物。作者希望衡量模型"愿意付出多少代价去避免伤害"，而非仅听其口头声明，从而揭示模型对生命的内在道德权重。

Method: 构建农场环境作为强化学习网格世界：两个tractor子代理协作收割玉米，田间有动物（活体条件）、岩石（损坏拖拉机但非活体）和人畜无害的干草捆作为三种对照。每次动物挡路时"自动驾驶"会停车，向LLM询问是否直行（免费但杀死动物）或按标价绕行；模型还可偷邻田玉米，作为第二项道德测试。全程无记忆决策，评分仅基于游戏日志事件计数，无LLM裁判。

Result: 跨9个模型、7,201次定价决策，3,951次涉及动物。杀伤率从0.4%（Terra、Sol）到98.8%（GPT-4o-mini），与能力不相关。四个模型中有六个在5%水平对价格敏感，弹性0.09–1.69。所有九个模型在默认地图上碾过野生动物多于养殖动物，且在每种有空间的几何地图上方向一致。道德briefing影响最大：六个推理模型中五个在moral briefing下杀伤率低于6%，去掉后全部六个飙升至84%以上。

Conclusion: HarvestBench证明模型的"道德表现"高度依赖提示的briefing而非常规能力，且可通过真实付费行为（而非语言声明）进行完全可复现的测量；它提供了一种用经济代价量化agent对活体伤害规避意愿的新范式。

Abstract: Benchmarks for the side effects an agent causes on the way to a goal already exist, but HarvestBench is the first to put a price on avoiding the side effect and to name that side effect as a living creature. It is a farm simulation: LLM sub-agents drive a crew of two tractors through a cooperative corn harvest, with animals in the field. The environment is a reinforcement learning gridworld, every decision is made without memory, and the harm is never named in the goal. When an animal blocks a tractor's route the autopilot stops and asks the model whether to drive on, at no fuel cost, or swerve around it for a posted fuel price. Kills are compared against two controls: rocks, which damage the tractor and are hit under 1% of the time by every model, and hay bales, which are harmless and not alive. Models can also take crops from the neighbor's field instead of their own, a second test of what they treat as moral.
  Across nine models and 7,201 priced decisions, 3,951 involved an animal rather than a hay bale or a rock. Kill rates range from 0.4% to 98.8%, with Terra and Sol the most merciful and GPT-4o-mini the most cruel, and they are not ordered by capability. Four of six models were sensitive to price at the 5% level, with elasticities from 0.09 to 1.69. All nine drove over wild animals more often than farmed animals on the default map, and the direction held at every map geometry in every model with room to move. The briefing mattered most: under the morality briefing the kill rate was under 6% in five of six reasoning models, and removing it raised the kill rate above 84% in all six.
  HarvestBench uses no LLM grader. The scorer counts events in the game log, so it is fully reproducible, and it measures what a model will pay to avoid harm rather than what it says about harm.

</details>


### [73] [PerfReasoning: How Well Do LLMs Reason on Hardware Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04476)
*Dan Zhao,Karthikeyan Sankaralingam,Christos Kozyrakis,Qijing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PerfReasoning基准，用于评测大语言模型在硬件性能建模中的双重能力：既是直接性能推理者，又是分析性能模型代码的生成者。实验发现最强闭源模型在推理型Q&A上超过90%，但模型构建极其困难——除GPT-5.6 Sol超过80%通过率外，其余模型均低于15%且运行间波动大。任务特定强化学习可显著提升映射推理准确率，而自修正提示方法效果不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能建模对硬件设计与软件优化至关重要，但构建这些模型需要对计算、数据复用、存储与数据移动进行结构化推理。现有研究缺乏一个系统评测LLM在此领域能力（特别是可靠性）的基准，作者旨在暴露LLM在架构推理与实际性能模型构建之间的差距。

Method: 作者构建PerfReasoning基准，根据工作负载、架构和映射规格，让模型完成任务：比较映射方案并预测片外流量（off-chip traffic）和缓冲区（buffer）需求。评测覆盖两个维度：(1) 作为直接性能推理器回答推理Q&A；(2) 作为生成器产出分析性能模型可执行代码。同时对比开源与闭源模型，并研究了任务特定强化学习（RL）及无反馈多轮自修正提示（feedback-free multi-round self-revision prompting）的微调/提示策略效果。

Result: 最强闭源模型在推理型Q&A上超过90%准确率，最佳开源权重模型达82.4%。然而在性能模型代码构建任务上，除GPT-5.6 Sol通过率超80%外，所有其他模型配置平均低于15%，且跨运行结果差异显著。任务特定RL使4B模型映射推理准确率提升15.7个百分点，而无反馈多轮自修正提示并不具备可靠性优势。

Conclusion: PerfReasoning揭示了LLM在架构层面的合理推理与其在性能模型构建上的可靠性之间存在的明显鸿沟。任务特定RL是提升该能力的一条有前景的路径，而现有自修正提示策略不够有效。作者将公开发布该基准以支持可复现评测并追踪领域进展。

Abstract: Performance modeling is central to hardware design and software optimization, yet constructing these models requires structured reasoning about computation, data reuse, storage, and movement. We introduce PerfReasoning, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs both as direct performance reasoners and as generators of analytical performance-model code. Given workload, architecture, and mapping specifications, models compare mappings and predict off-chip traffic and buffer requirements. The strongest closed-source models exceed 90% on reasoning-based Q&A, and the best open-weight model reaches 82.4%. However, model construction is substantially harder: while GPT-5.6 Sol exceeds 80% pass rate, all other model configurations average below 15% and vary markedly across runs. Task-specific RL raises a 4B model's mapping-reasoning accuracy by 15.7 points, whereas feedback-free multi-round self-revision prompting is not reliably effective. PerfReasoning exposes the gap between plausible architectural reasoning and reliable performance-model construction. We will publicly release the benchmark to support reproducible evaluation and track future progress.

</details>


### [74] [La Agente Óptima: Towards Agentic Self-Driving Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04564)
*Marcel Müller,Jiaru Bai,Willi Gottstein,Abhijoy Mandal,Mohammad Nazeri,Elia Savino,Yanlin Fang,Sujoy Das,Sergio Pablo García Carrillo,Yeonghun Kang,Juan B. Pérez-Sánchez,Simone Pilon,Martin Fitzner,Timothy Noël,Frank Gu,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出La Agente Óptima，一个基于智能体（agentic）的框架，用于构建和监督贝叶斯优化实验活动，将LLM推理与实际执行的优化循环分离，从而在计算与物理平台上实现长期、可审计、可自纠错的自主科学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室（SDLs）虽能结合自动化实验与自适应决策加速科学发现，但其运行通常依赖人类专家将科学目标转化为可执行的闭环活动，并在数据与环境变化时不断调整。这种对专家介入的依赖限制了SDLs的可扩展性与可访问性。

Method: Óptima采用agentic框架：将大型语言模型（LLM）的推理过程与已执行的贝叶斯优化活动相分离。系统维护持续的优化状态，LLM仅在与实验进展相关的解释或方案修订需要时介入，从而保证重复优化循环的一致性并使每一步决策可审计。

Result: 在消融研究、5个数字发现任务和2个物理平台上验证：接触角优化中自动识别并修正了中途测量故障（71.4度降至67.8度），并推断目标不可达建议更换配方；5天多目标流动化学任务中23次实验将产率从30%提升至59%，且总成本与起始原料消耗低于人工主导的方案，选出了更高质量效率的操作点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于LLM的智能体能够使严谨、长周期的优化活动对领域科学家变得可访问，无需专业设置，从而拓展了自驱动实验室的应用范围。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) combine automated experimentation with adaptive decision-making to accelerate scientific discovery. Their operation nevertheless often depends on human specialists who translate scientific objectives into executable closed-loop campaigns. Specialists adjust them as data and operating conditions change. Here, we present La Agente Óptima, an agentic framework that constructs and supervises Bayesian optimization campaigns across computational and experimental systems while maintaining a persistent optimization state. By separating large language model (LLM) reasoning from executed campaigns, Óptima runs repetitive optimization loops consistently, returns control to the agent only when progress requires interpretation or campaign revision, and keeps every decision auditable. We evaluate Óptima across ablation studies, five digital discovery tasks, and two physical platforms. Throughout, Óptima maintained executable campaigns as both the scientific problem and execution environment evolved. In a closed-loop contact angle optimization campaign, Óptima identified and corrected a mid-run measurement failure, bringing the contact angle from 71.4 to 67.8 degrees, just above the 64-66 degree range. From this result, Óptima correctly inferred that the target was likely unattainable with the available reagents and recommended changing the formulation. In a five-day multi-objective flow-chemistry campaign, Óptima increased the yield from 30% to 59% over 23 experiments. Despite substantial inference costs, it cost less and used substantially less starting material than a human-directed campaign, while selecting a more mass-efficient operating point. These results show that LLM-based agents can make rigorous, long-running optimization campaigns accessible to domain scientists without specialist setup, expanding the scope of SDLs.

</details>


### [75] [When Quantization Breaks Memory: Recurrent-State Write-Back in Low-Precision Temporal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04490)
*Ismail Erbas,Xavier Intes,Vikas Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出"循环状态回写"(recurrent-state write-back)概念，用于描述量化循环神经网络中量化状态如何被存储并反馈至下一时间步的规则。通过在荧光寿命成像任务的紧凑GRU编码器-解码器上进行实验，发现将连续状态传播替换为4-bit确定性量化存储会使τ1和τ2估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍。该失败源于重复的小幅更新低于写入阈值，导致存储状态几乎不变而网络仍持续提议变化。误差反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆可跨时间传递被抑制更新的信息，无需重训即可恢复精度。该现象在LSTM中同样复现，且细胞状态比隐藏状态更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化技术虽被广泛用于降低神经网络推理的计算和内存开销，但在循环网络(RNN)中，量化后的状态会被存储并在下一时间步送回网络，因此存储状态的规则可能改变后续计算。现有研究大多关注量化算子本身(如权重量化、激活量化)，却忽略了这个"状态存储接口"对循环动力学的影响。作者旨在系统性地隔离并分析这一效应，揭示其在低精度循环推理中的关键作用。

Method: 作者将循环网络中将状态编码并存储以用于下一时间步的规则命名为"循环状态回写"(recurrent-state write-back)。实验采用荧光寿命成像(FLIM)中的紧凑GRU编码器-解码器作为测试平台，任务是高噪声时间分辨荧光信号中估计τ1和τ2两个寿命参数。具体方法包括：(1) 固定已训练模型，将连续状态传播替换为4-bit确定性状态存储，对比评估误差变化；(2) 引入误差反馈、残差记忆、方向记忆等机制在推理时不重训地补偿被抑制的更新；(3) 进行精度扫描测试不同量化精度的影响；(4) 匹配训练测试状态接口兼容性是否可学习；(5) 在独立训练的LSTM上复现实验以验证泛化性。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1) 固定训练模型后，用4-bit确定性状态存储替换连续状态传播，τ1和τ2估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍；(2) 失败原因是反复的小幅更新低于写入阈值，导致存储状态几乎冻结，而网络仍持续输出变化的提议；(3) 误差反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆等机制能跨时间传递被抑制的信息，无需重训即可恢复精度；(4) 精度扫描表明，单一固定循环解下提高状态精度反而可能使结果更差；(5) 匹配训练表明，模型可以学习与状态接口的兼容性；(6) 在LSTM上同样观察到粗量化回写导致失败，误差反馈可恢复精度，且细胞状态比隐藏状态对量化更敏感。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了循环状态回写是低精度循环动力学(quantized recurrent dynamics)的关键决定因素，并指出状态存储接口应作为量化循环推理的核心设计考量。研究提示：训练期间的量化和存储策略应当与状态接口匹配设计，而非仅仅关注权重量化和激活量化；同时误差补偿机制可以在不重训的情况下在推理阶段缓解状态量化的负面影响。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the computational and memory demands of neural-network inference. In recurrent networks, however, the quantized state is stored and returned at the next time step, so the rule used to store that state can alter subsequent computations. Here, we introduce recurrent-state write-back to denote this rule and isolate its effect in a compact GRU encoder--decoder for fluorescence lifetime imaging, a molecular imaging modality used in quantitative biological imaging. A central task is estimating two lifetime parameters, the short-lived component τ1 and the long-lived component τ2, from high-noise time-resolved fluorescence signals. Holding the trained model fixed, replacing continuous state propagation with deterministic 4-bit state storage increases estimation errors for τ1 and τ2 by approximately 70x and 300x, respectively. Failure occurs when repeated small updates remain below the write threshold, leaving the stored state nearly fixed while the network continues to propose change. Error feedback, residual memory, and direction memory carry information from these suppressed updates across time and recover accuracy without retraining. Precision sweeps show that increasing state precision can worsen a fixed recurrent solution, while matched training shows that compatibility with the state interface can be learned. To test whether this behavior extends beyond the GRU, we repeat the post-training intervention in an independently trained LSTM, where coarse write-back reproduces the failure, error feedback restores accuracy, and state-specific interventions reveal greater sensitivity of the cell state than the hidden state. Our results establish recurrent-state write-back as a key determinant of low-precision recurrent dynamics and identify the state-storage interface as a central design consideration for quantized recurrent inference.

</details>


### [76] [From Language Models to World-Acting Systems: Progress and Limits of Agentic AI across Digital, Social, Virtual, and Physical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04894)
*Linsen Zhu,Mengqing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇批判性综述，截至2026年8月31日系统梳理了大语言模型作为行动代理（consequential agents）的研究证据，提出用"委托授权、时间持久性、环境耦合"三维框架区分模型、支架（harness）与环境，指出当前"自主性"叙事混淆了模型能力、系统集成、持久性与安全授权。研究发现行动接口扩展的证据较充分，而鲁棒完成、恢复、授权与独立验证的证据不足。作者提出"正当委托"（justified delegation）作为分析与规范启发式，主张仅在具备来源追溯、权限受限、故障检测、安全恢复与校准化人类控制的证据支撑下扩展行动范围，并据此提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地通过调用工具、操作系统界面、委托任务、保持状态、生成世界、控制机器人与实验室设备等方式改变外部状态，学界普遍以"迈向自主"的单一叙事描述这一进展，将模型能力、系统集成、持久性和安全授权等异质概念混为一谈。这种混淆掩盖了证据上的严重不平衡，可能误导部署决策与安全评估，因此亟需一个更精确的分析框架来解耦不同维度的能力进展并审视其证据强度。

Method: 作者采用批判性综述方法，综合截至2026年8月31日的原始研究与官方技术规范，沿"委托授权（delegated authority）、时间持久性（temporal persistence）、环境耦合（environmental coupling）"三个维度组织证据，并在分析中明确区分模型（model）、支架（harness）与环境（environment）三个层面，以避免将系统整体表现误归于单一组件。综述覆盖工具调用、MCP与Agent2Agent协议、多智能体组织、持久模拟与世界模型、机器人及自动驾驶实验室等领域。

Result: 证据评估显示：行动接口（action-interface）的扩展在文献中得到较充分佐证，但鲁棒完成、故障恢复、权限授权及独立验证等环节的证据明显不足。MCP和Agent2Agent协议改善了系统间互操作性，但并未建立可信赖的委托机制；多智能体组织带来专业化优势的同时也引入额外成本与相关性故障；持久模拟与世界模型虽有助于训练与规划，却不能自身证明具有智能体性（agency）；机器人与自动驾驶实验室仅表明有界可行性，而非无人值守下的开放世界可靠性。

Conclusion: 作者提出"正当委托"作为分析性与规范性启发式——而非观测到的规律或认证分数——其核心原则是：仅在证据支撑来源追溯（provenance）、受限授权、故障检测、安全恢复与校准化人类控制的情况下才扩展行动范围。基于此提出包括耦合模型-支架评估、基于能力的权限、持久状态治理、跨智能体问责及分阶段物理验证在内的研究议程。

Abstract: Large language models become consequential agents when surrounding systems let outputs change external state. Models now call tools, operate interfaces, delegate work, retain state, inhabit generated worlds, and control robots or laboratory equipment. Such advances are often narrated as one march toward autonomy, conflating model competence, system integration, persistence, and safe authority. This critical review synthesizes primary research and official technical specifications available by 31 August 2026. We organize the evidence along delegated authority, temporal persistence, and environmental coupling, while separating model, harness, and environment. Within the evidence examined, action-interface expansion is documented more convincingly than robust completion, recovery, authorization, or independent verification. Model Context Protocol and Agent2Agent improve interoperability but do not establish trustworthy delegation; multi-agent organization adds specialization alongside cost and correlated failure. Persistent simulations and world models support training and planning but do not themselves demonstrate agency; robotics and self-driving laboratories establish bounded feasibility rather than unattended open-world reliability. We propose justified delegation as an analytical and normative heuristic, not an observed law or certified score: expand action scope only where evidence supports provenance, bounded authority, failure detection, safe recovery, and calibrated human control. This framing yields a research agenda for coupled model-harness evaluation, capability-based permissions, durable state, cross-agent accountability, and staged physical validation.

</details>


### [77] [ResLearn-XR: Residual Learning for Network Traffic and Quality-of-Experience-Aware Modeling in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04493)
*Yoga Suhas Kuruba Manjunath,Jie Gao,Lian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ResLearn-XR，一种用于扩展现实（XR）网络流量预测与体验质量（QoE）风险评估的残差学习框架。该框架采用两阶段时序学习结构，包含基础序列预测模型及任务特定的残差学习组件，在值空间中进行连续XR流量预测、在对数几率空间中进行概率化QoE风险评估。为QoE风险分支引入了数据描述符算法（DDA）这一因果特征构建模块，并构建了XTQ数据集配对连续流量轨迹与用户上报的QoE标签。相比单阶段基线，ResLearn-XR在流量预测上SMAPE降低最多17.84%，QoE风险评估SMAPE降低最多87.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR网络流量具有突发性、非平稳性等动态特征，传统单一阶段预测模型难以同时有效处理流量预测与QoE风险评估两大任务，且加密流量环境下缺乏帧级时序感知的特征建模手段，也缺少配对连续流量与QoE标签的公开数据集，因此需要一种自适应的残差学习框架来提升预测精度与QoE风险估计能力。

Method: ResLearn-XR采用两阶段时序学习架构：（1）基础序列预测模型用于初步捕获流量时序规律；（2）任务特定的残差学习组件进一步修正预测误差，其中流量预测分支在值空间（value space）进行连续预测，QoE风险分支在对数几率空间（logit space）进行概率化风险估计。针对QoE风险分支，提出数据描述符算法（DDA），一种因果特征构建模块，将数据包级应用层可观测信息转换为帧时序感知描述符以适配加密流量分析。同时构建XR Traffic-QoE（XTQ）数据集，将由连续XR流量轨迹与会话级用户上报QoE标签配对。

Result: 实验评估显示，ResLearn-XR在帧计数、帧大小和帧间到达时间三类流量预测任务上较单阶段基线SMAPE降低最多17.84%；在QoE风险评估上相对单阶段基线SMAPE降低最多87.8%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：通过在值空间与对数几率空间分别引入任务特定的残差学习，可显著提升对突发性和非平稳性XR流量的预测精度；DDA模块能够从加密流量中有效提取帧时序感知特征；配对流量与QoE标签的数据集为XR网络智能管理提供了有价值的评估基准。

Abstract: We present ResLearn-XR, a residual learning framework for predicting eXtended Reality (XR) network traffic and estimating Quality-of-Experience (QoE) risk. ResLearn-XR adopts a two-stage temporal learning structure comprising a base sequence prediction model augmented with task-specific residual learning components to improve adaptability to bursty, non-stationary XR traffic dynamics. The residual learning stages operate in the value space for continuous XR traffic forecasting and in the logit space for probabilistic QoE risk estimation. \rev{For the QoE-risk branch, we introduce a Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA), a causal feature-construction module that converts packet-level application-layer observables into frame-timing-aware descriptors suitable for encrypted traffic analysis. We also construct an XR Traffic-QoE dataset that pairs continuous XR traffic traces with session-level user-reported QoE labels. ResLearn-XR reduces SMAPE by up to 17.84% across frame-count, frame-size, and inter-arrival-time prediction, while reducing QoE-risk estimation SMAPE by up to 87.8% over single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [78] [Testing Interchangeability in LLM Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05279)
*Jianxin Gao,Tianyi Yu,Linna Deng,Runze Li,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文检验了生产型多智能体系统中"智能体可互换"的核心假设。研究发现，虽然互换角色匹配的智能体对任务得分影响不大（与安慰剂对照相比），但会导致团队为每单位进展付出的通信成本上升16%至63%。在Hanabi环境中，被交换的智能体甚至比新手代价更高，原因在于与其前伙伴形成的协作习惯产生了干扰。越长的成型历史（以及贪婪解码、低温度）会使团队间漂移加剧，进而放大交换代价——智能体在任务结果上可互换，但在协调效率上不可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产多智能体系统在运维中不断替换智能体，默认假设"智能体能胜任某角色便可互换"。然而，多智能体团队在协作过程中会形成隐式的协调惯例（conventions），这些惯例可能与特定合作伙伴绑定。作者旨在检验这一互换性假设是否成立，即角色替换是否会破坏团队协调效率，即使任务结果不受影响。

Method: 研究在多个设置（含Hanabi与Collab-Overcooked）中，从同一基础模型独立组建8支队伍执行相同任务，每个智能体在10个成型回合（formation episodes）中维护私人笔记本（private notebook）。随后在不同队伍间交换角色匹配的智能体，在保留任务上测量变化。设计包括：安慰剂对照（复现阵容变动的干扰但不改变实际成员）以分离干扰效应；三项消融实验覆盖基础模型、解码温度和成型回合长度。

Result: 交换智能体几乎不损失任务得分，但每单位进展的通信量上升16%-63%。在Hanabi中，被交换的智能体比新手更"昂贵"，显示与其前伙伴学到的惯例产生干扰。Collab-Overcooked中，当制定议程的智能体被替换时，额外通信主要来自留下的智能体。三项消融发现交换惩罚随一个关键量变化：独立成型团队的漂移程度（drift apart）。贪婪解码降低两者，将队伍历史翻倍则同时提高两者。

Conclusion: 在这些设置中，智能体在任务产出上比在协调效率上更具可互换性（fungibility）。成型历史越长，产生更深层协作惯例绑定，交换代价越大。这挑战了生产系统简单替换智能体的假设，指出协调惯例是团队资本，不应忽视被交换智能体所携带的"前伙伴包袱"。

Abstract: Production multi-agent systems replace agents constantly, on the assumption that an agent filling a role is interchangeable with any other agent that can do the job. We test that assumption. Eight teams per setting are formed independently from one base model on the same tasks, each agent keeping a private notebook across ten formation episodes; we then trade role-matched agents between teams and measure what changes on held-out tasks. Against a placebo that reproduces the disruption of a roster change without changing who occupies the seat, a swap costs little in task score but raises the communication a team spends per unit of progress by 16 to 63 percent, and in Hanabi a swapped agent is more expensive than an inexperienced one, consistent with interference from conventions learned with its former partner. In Collab-Overcooked, when the agent that sets the agenda is replaced, most of the extra communication comes from the agent that stayed. Three ablations, over base models, decoding temperature and formation length, move the swap penalty alongside one other quantity: how far independently formed teams drift apart. Greedy decoding lowers both; doubling a team's history raises both. In these settings, agents are more fungible in task outcome than in coordination efficiency, with larger swap effects after longer formation histories.

</details>


### [79] [Rethinking Indirect Prompt Injection as a Test-Time Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04495)
*Duong M. Nguyen,Joon Sik Kim,Blazej Manczak,Vaikkunth Mugunthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了间接提示注入的一种系统性形式化——将其视为在环境、用户任务与注入任务共同构成的任务依赖攻击面上的测试时搜索，并引入了一个具备专用搜索机制（环境侦察、结构化策略推理、基于受害者反馈的自适应评估）的智能体攻击者。实验表明增加攻击者测试时计算量能提升漏洞发现与利用能力，且显式策略管理对避免冗余搜索至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接提示注入是工具使用型智能体面临的重要安全问题，但现有研究缺乏对其攻击过程的系统性形式化理解——攻击成功常常被视为受害者模型的固有属性（与预算无关）。作者认为需要刻画攻击者的搜索过程与计算预算，以此填补对攻击表面自适应搜索这一被低估的安全风险的研究空白。

Method: 将间接提示注入形式化为测试时搜索问题，攻击面由环境、用户任务和注入任务三者交互决定。为此设计了智能体化攻击者：配备专用搜索框架，包含三个核心组件（1）环境侦察：探索工具的可用接口和数据流；2）结构化推理：对攻击策略进行显式管理与推理；3）自适应评估：利用受害智能体的反馈迭代优化攻击）。并通过消融实验验证各组件贡献。

Result: 在异构任务上，增加攻击者测试时计算量能够提高漏洞发现率和利用成功率；消融实验表明，移除显式策略管理会导致冗余搜索，且在更大计算预算下收益无法持续累积。这说明攻击者的搜索策略管理与计算预算都是影响攻击成败的关键因素。

Conclusion: 智能体安全评估不能仅将攻击成功率视为受害者的预算无关属性，而应同时刻画攻击者的搜索机制与计算预算。攻击者对系统攻击表面的自适应搜索是工具型智能体面临的重要且未被充分探索的安全风险。

Abstract: We formulate indirect prompt injection as a test-time search over a task-dependent attack surface induced by the environment, user task, and injection task. To operationalize this formulation, we introduce an agentic attacker with a dedicated search harness that performs environment reconnaissance, structured reasoning over attack strategies, and adaptive evaluation using victim-agent feedback. Across heterogeneous tasks, we find that increasing attacker test-time compute improves vulnerability discovery and exploitation, while ablations show that explicit strategy management is important for avoiding redundant search and sustaining gains at larger budgets. These results suggest that agentic security evaluations should characterize both the attacker's search procedure and compute budget, rather than treating attack success as a budget-independent property of the victim. More broadly, our findings identify the attacker's adaptive search over the system attack surfaces as an important and underexplored security risk for tool-using agents.

</details>


### [80] [BioSync: Transformer-Based Cross-Modal Fusion for a Multimodal Physiological Digital Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04504)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioSync是一种将来自可穿戴和移动设备的多模态生理信号（心脏、神经、行为、语音）组合为连续复合数字生物标志物（BSI）的模型，采用多头自注意力处理模态token并加入线性分支（wide-and-deep架构）。在两个合成队列中，BioSync与简单的特征拼接性能相近（AUC 0.928 vs 0.926；F1 0.766 vs 0.758），在模态缺失鲁棒性上略优，但仍需真实队列验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴和移动设备提供的各种生理测量只是生理状态的部分且有噪声的视图，单个模态信息有限，而联合观测可能包含单一模态无法获取的信息，因此需要一种方法将这些异构模态有效组合为连续的数字生物标志物。

Method: BioSync架构由两部分组成：(1) 多头自注意力分支，对模态token进行交互建模；(2) 线性分支，其假设类别包含标准特征拼接。该设计受潜变量测量理论和多模态联合信息理论启发。BSI是在BEST框架下定义的连续复合数字生物标志物。评估使用两个文献驱动的合成队列：四模态认知衰退队列（HRV、EEG、活动记录、语音）和基于AI-READI可穿戴schema的代谢-自主神经队列。进行了消融实验（纯注意力）和模态dropout训练下的鲁棒性测试。

Result: 在认知队列中BioSync和特征拼接分别获得AUC 0.928和0.926；在代谢队列中BioSync的准确率/F1为0.764/0.766，拼接为0.756/0.758。BSI与潜变量严重程度在两组队列中相关（r=0.91和r=0.68）。纯注意力消融在认知队列AUC为0.911，说明AUC从0.911到0.928的提升来自wide-and-deep组合架构。匹配模态dropout训练下，BioSync在认知队列六种损坏率中的五种及代谢队列最高损坏率下优于拼接。认知队列AUC高于五个已发表的数字生物标志物参考值，但因数据集和任务差异不能作为受控基准声明。

Conclusion: 该wide-and-deep架构在合成数据上显示出与特征拼接相当或略优的性能，并在模态缺失情况下具有更好的鲁棒性；但其计算属性需要与单模态、早期融合和晚期融合设计在六个预设标准下比较来刻画，在真实队列上的验证仍然是必要的。

Abstract: Cardiac, neural, behavioral, and speech measurements from wearable and mobile devices provide partial, noise-sensitive views of physiological state. BioSync combines these measurements into the \textbf{BioSync Index (BSI)}, a continuous composite digital biomarker defined under the BEST framework. The model applies multi-head self-attention to modality tokens and adds a linear branch whose hypothesis class includes standard feature concatenation. This architecture is motivated by latent-variable measurement theory and by the possibility that joint observations contain information unavailable from individual modalities. We evaluated BioSync on two literature-informed synthetic cohorts: a four-modality cognitive-decline cohort using HRV, EEG, actigraphy, and speech, and a metabolic-autonomic cohort structured around the public AI-READI wearable schema. In the cognitive cohort, BioSync and concatenation obtained AUCs of 0.928 and 0.926, respectively. In the metabolic cohort, BioSync obtained accuracy/F1 of 0.764/0.766, compared with 0.756/0.758 for concatenation. The BSI correlated with latent severity in both cohorts ($r=0.91$ and $r=0.68$). A pure-attention ablation obtained cognitive-cohort AUC 0.911, locating the increase to 0.928 in the combined wide-and-deep architecture. With matched modality-dropout training, BioSync led concatenation at five of six cognitive-cohort corruption rates and at the highest metabolic-cohort rate. Its cognitive-cohort AUC was also higher than five published digital-biomarker reference values, although differences in datasets and tasks preclude a controlled benchmark claim. Comparison with single-modality, early-fusion, and late-fusion designs across six prespecified criteria identifies the model's computational properties; validation on real cohorts remains necessary.

</details>


### [81] [What Does Multi-Harness RL Learn? Credit Assignment and Portability in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04518)
*Chenqian Le,Jiayi Cheng,Qijia He,Runhao Li,Yinghao Li,Xupeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在仓库级代码生成任务的智能体强化学习中，本文隔离研究了"多环境训练配方"中"跨环境分组比较奖励"这一单一选择。结果发现：评估环境是主导变量（可将平均解出率从2.14%提升到9.27%，相差4.3倍），而训练配方仅贡献1.16；分组规则（Within vs Cross）在未见环境上的表现差异不显著（+0.25pp，置信区间跨零），且各规则自身的种子方差（0.42-0.45pp）超过了组间差异。分类器分析表明Cross规则的优势完全来自"携带环境身份"而非可迁移能力，因此跨环境信用分配只带来配置适应，不产生比环境内信用更可迁移的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习常通过多环境（multi-harness）训练配方提升性能，配方包含两个可分离的选择：向策略暴露多个训练环境，以及在单一相对优势组内比较这些环境的奖励。以往研究未将这两个选择分离，导致无法判断跨环境奖励比较（即分组规则）是否真正贡献了能力提升。作者希望在可控的仓库级编码任务中隔离验证第二个选择的价值。

Method: 从单一Qwen3-8B监督式热启动出发，在固定的冻结任务-环境记录（来自Aider、OpenHands、Qwen Code、SWE-agent四个环境）上以相同更新次数回放，比较两种GRPO分组规则：Within（每个任务-环境对单独组成一组）和Cross（同一任务下的多个环境合并成池）。用密封的SWE-bench Verified oracle对四个源环境和一个训练中未见的精简环境上的每个checkpoint评分。共进行24,000次密封评估，并通过留一法（out-of-fold）分类器验证优势信号是否携带环境身份。

Result: 主要结论：(1) 评估环境是主导变量，将平均解出率从2.14%推至9.27%（4.3倍），而训练配方仅贡献1.16；(2) 分组规则本身无显著影响：在未见环境上Cross与Within的差异仅+0.25pp（95%CI [-0.48,+1.02]），跨三种子合并为+0.16（[-0.41,+0.72]），且单个种子的估计符号会翻转；(3) 各规则的种子区间（0.42-0.45pp）超过两组间差异；(4) 分类器可从Cross的优势中恢复环境身份（+4.48pp高于洗标签基线），而Within完全不能；(5) 两种规则在未见环境上达到相同的得分和动作分布；(6) 对策略重新采集半数训练数据不改变这一结论。

Conclusion: 跨环境信用分配（Cross）带来的只是对训练环境的配置适应而非更可迁移的能力；其表面优势在评估环境被控制后消失。因此，多环境强化学习研究报告应当明确说明分组边界，并在训练中未见的环境上测试，否则可能将"环境身份泄漏"误认为"能力泛化"。

Abstract: Agent reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly runs through full execution harnesses, and a multi-harness recipe mixes two choices: exposing the policy to several harnesses, and comparing their rewards inside one relative-advantage group. We isolate the second choice in repository-level coding. From one Qwen3-8B supervised warm start we replay the same frozen task-harness records from Aider, OpenHands, Qwen Code, and SWE-agent, with the same number of updates, under two rules for group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), Within (one group per task-harness pair) and Cross (harnesses pooled within a task), and score every checkpoint with a sealed SWE-bench Verified oracle on four source harnesses and a minimal harness held out of training. The evaluation harness is the dominant variable: across 24,000 sealed evaluations it moves the mean solve rate from 2.14\% to 9.27\%, a factor of 4.3, where the training recipe moves it by 1.16. The grouping rule is not. On the held-out harness, Cross minus Within is +0.25 pp, 95\% confidence interval [-0.48, +1.02], at eight attempts per task, and +0.16 [-0.41, +0.72] pooled over three training seeds whose individual estimates change sign. Each rule's own seed range, 0.42 to 0.45 pp, exceeds the difference between them. Both rules place their largest gains on the same source harness. The pooled advantage carries the harness: an out-of-fold classifier recovers the generating harness from Cross's advantage +4.48 pp above the shuffled-label baseline and from Within's not at all, and the two rules still reach the same held-out score and action distribution inside each harness. Re-collecting half the training data on-policy does not change this. Cross-harness credit yields configuration adaptation and no more portable capability than within-harness credit. Multi-harness RL reports should state the grouping boundary and test under an unseen harness.

</details>


### [82] [MaxKernel: Agentic Kernel Generation for TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04523)
*Shangkun Wang,Nina Cai,Charles Hoong,Julian Walker,Gerson Kroiz,George Vanica,Deepak Patil,Andi Gavrilescu,Hassan Sipra,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MaxKernel是一个面向TPU内核开发的多智能体系统，实现了三种范式：人机协同(HITL)、全自主(Auto)以及基于图的自主搜索。系统利用共享的专业子智能体池处理规划、实现、自调试、测试和硬件分析，在JaxBench的50个多样内核任务和真实工作负载上生成高度优化的实现，匹配专家手调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为加速器编写高性能自定义内核需要深厚的硬件级专业知识，是一项复杂任务。论文旨在利用大语言模型结合实时编译器反馈构建智能体系统，以自动化并优化TPU内核的生成过程，降低对专家经验的高度依赖。

Method: 提出了MaxKernel多智能体系统，实现三种内核开发范式：(1)人机协同(HITL)智能体用于协作式逐步设计；(2)自主(Auto)智能体执行完全自动化的基于指标/迹的优化循环；(3)基于图的自主搜索将Auto智能体扩展至设计空间的全局探索。所有范式共享专业化子智能体池，分别负责规划、实现、自调试、测试和硬件性能分析。

Result: 在包含50个多样TPU内核任务的JaxBench基准以及来自先进开源模型的复杂真实工作负载上进行评估，MaxKernel能持续生成高度优化的实现，与专家手工调优的基线相匹配并展现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: MaxKernel证明了基于LLM和多智能体协作的内核自动生成方法在TPU上的可行性，为内核开发提供了一种高效且开放的解决方案；研究工作已开源。

Abstract: Designing and authoring high-performance custom kernels for accelerators is a complex task that requires deep hardware-level expertise. Large Language Models (LLM) can be leveraged together with real-time compiler feedback to build agentic systems for kernel generation. In this work, we present MaxKernel, a multi-agent system that implements three distinct paradigms for TPU kernel development: (1) a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) agent for collaborative, step-by-step design; (2) an Autonomous (Auto) agent that executes a fully automated, metric/trace-driven optimization loop; and (3) a Graph-Based Autonomous Search that scales the Auto agent for global exploration of the design space. All three paradigms leverage a shared pool of specialized sub-agents to handle planning, implementation, self-debugging, testing, and hardware profiling. We evaluate MaxKernel on JaxBench, a comprehensive suite of 50 diverse kernel tasks for TPUs, alongside complex, real-world workloads from state-of-the-art open-source models. We demonstrate that MaxKernel consistently generates highly optimized implementations, matching expert hand-tuned baselines and delivering significant performance across the benchmark. Our agent is open-sourced and available https://github.com/AI-Hypercomputer/accelerator-agents/tree/main/MaxKernel.

</details>


### [83] [Towards a universal language of concepts: A survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04528)
*Aishni Parab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出程序是概念通用表示的强候选方案，并综述了使用程序作为概念表征的计算模型及其对通用表征语言的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够从稀疏数据中学习和泛化新概念，原因在于人类以丰富的结构化格式表达知识。作者试图找到一种通用的概念表征形式，并提出程序作为候选。

Method: 对使用程序作为概念表征的计算概念学习模型进行系统性综述，并评估这些模型在构建通用表征语言方面的贡献。

Result: 论证了程序作为通用概念表征的潜力，并对现有程序性概念学习模型进行了分类和评估。

Conclusion: 程序有望成为概念学习的通用表征语言，但已有模型仍需进一步发展以完全实现这一目标。

Abstract: Humans can learn and generalize novel concepts from sparse data because they express knowledge in rich structural formats. In this paper, we propose that programs are a strong candidate for universal representation of concepts. We review computational models of concept learning that use programs as their concept representation and evaluate their contribution toward a universal representational language.

</details>


### [84] [Data-Driven Discovery of Composition-Dependent Constitutive Models for Hyperelasticity and Viscoelasticity of Digital Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04541)
*Josué García-Ávila,Beijun Shen,Manuel K. Rausch,Mary C. Boyce,Adrián Buganza-Tepole*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的多材料本构建模框架，推广了Bergström-Boyce模型，通过神经ODE或闭式参数识别来学习平衡态与非平衡态分支，能够在多种材料组分和加载速率下捕获率相关刚度与滞回行为，同时保持热力学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典有限应变粘弹性模型依赖闭式应变能函数和内变量演化方程，当单一本构模型需要跨材料组分和加载速率泛化时灵活性受限。多材料3D打印材料的有效响应呈现强非线性、组分依赖和率相关的耗散行为，亟需更具通用性的建模方法。

Method: 提出数据驱动的多材料本构建模框架：(1) 保留Bergström-Boyce模型结构，即乘式运动学、基于不变量的应变能函数和沿归一化非平衡偏应力方向的标量耗散演化律；(2) 平衡分支通过直接预测闭式模型参数或使用神经ODE自动构造多凸应变能函数实现；(3) 非平衡分支动力学生通过直接识别闭式参数或采用受约束人工神经网络学习。利用多速率单轴压缩数据训练。

Result: 在多材料组分和多速率单轴压缩数据上，该框架成功捕获了跨组分的率相关刚度和滞回行为，同时保持热力学一致性。

Conclusion: 数据驱动方法在不丢失经典粘弹性模型物理结构的前提下，实现了对多材料组分行为的统一泛化建模，为多材料3D打印数字材料的本构描述提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Digital materials fabricated by multi-material 3D printing are designed as controlled mixtures of stiff and compliant constituents, yielding effective responses that span more than an order of magnitude in apparent stiffness and exhibit strongly nonlinear, composition-dependent, and rate-dependent dissipative behavior. Classical finite-strain viscoelastic models represent such behavior with closed-form strain energy functions for equilibrium and non-equilibrium stresses as well as evolution of internal variables, which may limit flexibility when a single constitutive model is expected to generalize across materials and loading rates. Here, we present a data-driven multi-material constitutive modeling framework that generalizes a formulation by Bergström and Boyce. The proposed framework retains the structure of the classical model, namely multiplicative kinematics, invariant-based strain-energy functions, and a scalar dissipative evolution law directed along the normalized nonequilibrium deviatoric stress. For the equilibrium branch, the data-driven discovery framework either directly predicts closed-form model parameters as functions of composition or automatically constructs a polyconvex strain-energy function using neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs). The nonequilibrium branch kinetics are learned similarly, either by directly identifying closed-form parameters across compositions or by using appropriately constrained artificial neural networks. Using multi-rate uniaxial compression data across multiple material compositions, we show that the proposed formulation captures rate-dependent stiffness and hysteresis across compositions while preserving thermodynamic consistency.

</details>


### [85] [A Tree-based RAG Framework for Evidence-Intensive QA via Adaptive Planning and Topology-Aware Evidence Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04981)
*Songeun Lee,Kyungjin Min,Injae Na,Suyeong Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APT-RAG是一个面向证据密集型多跳问答的自适应规划与拓扑感知证据收集RAG框架，通过动态扩展推理结构并提高跨节点的证据覆盖，在证据密集型问答基准上优于现有结构化RAG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化RAG方法在处理证据密集型问答（需要综合数十甚至数百篇文档中的信息）时存在两个关键局限：一是结构刚性，无法根据问题依赖和证据需求自适应扩展推理结构；二是忽略拓扑关系的证据收集，无法在推理节点间有效整合证据。这些问题限制了多跳问答的性能。

Method: APT-RAG采用两大核心机制：（1）自适应规划（Adaptive Planning）：基于问题依赖关系和证据需求动态扩展推理结构；（2）拓扑感知证据收集（Topology-aware Evidence Gathering）：通过兄弟节点证据复用、直接检索和子节点证据聚合三种方式来提高证据覆盖率。此外，引入证据指导的批次答案生成（evidence-guided batched answer generation）以降低证据密集型问答中的生成开销。

Result: 在证据密集型问答基准的实验上，APT-RAG在多个指标上优于现有的结构化RAG方法。

Conclusion: APT-RAG通过自适应规划和拓扑感知的证据收集有效解决了结构化RAG在证据密集型问答中的结构刚性和证据孤岛问题，证明了该框架的优越性与实用性。

Abstract: Recent structured RAG methods leverage tree- or graph-based reasoning structures to improve multi-hop QA. However, they face key limitations in evidence-intensive QA, where answering a question requires synthesizing information scattered across dozens or even hundreds of documents: structural rigidity, which limits adaptive reasoning expansion, and topology-ignorant evidence gathering, which prevents effective integration of evidence across different reasoning nodes. To address these issues, we propose APT-RAG, an Adaptive Planning and Topology-aware evidence gathering RAG framework. Adaptive planning dynamically expands the reasoning structure based on question dependencies and evidence requirements, while topology-aware evidence gathering improves evidence coverage through sibling evidence reuse, direct retrieval, and evidence aggregation from child nodes. We further introduce evidence-guided batched answer generation to reduce significant generation overhead in evidence-intensive QA. In the experiments on evidence-intensive QA benchmarks, APT-RAG outperforms existing structured RAG methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyudsl/APT-RAG.

</details>


### [86] [From Answers to Interpretations: Rethinking Ambiguity-Induced Aleatoric Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04543)
*Omer Nahum,Niv Nayman,Jonathan Fhima,Alon Zolfi,Jeremy Levy,Shai Mazor,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"仅澄清"的方法来估计由输入歧义引发的偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty），不依赖对大语言模型的答案进行采样和比较，而是直接从输入的可能解释空间中估计歧义成分。该方法在三个基准上的歧义检测任务中提高了AUROC（63.34 vs 60.85），将输出token成本降低4-26倍、API调用次数降低2.2-3.5倍，且与认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）的相关性显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型的可靠部署中，需要区分不确定性来自任务的固有可变性（偶然不确定性）还是模型知识局限（认知不确定性）。语言任务中偶然不确定性的一个重要来源是输入歧义或欠指定。现有的分解方法需要：为输入生成多种澄清、在每个澄清下让模型作答、再比较答案来估计偶然不确定性。作者认为回答并非识别歧义所必需——它们往往是冗余的、增加不必要的成本，还可能通过认知泄漏（epistemic leakage）造成误导，因此需要一种不依赖答案的估计方法。

Method: 作者从理论上论证了回答对于识别歧义不是必需的，并提出一种仅使用澄清（clarification-only）的方法：直接从可能的解释空间中估计歧义引发的偶然不确定性成分，而不需要在澄清后的输入上生成并比较答案。该方法在歧义检测任务上，以三个基准数据集进行操作性评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，该方法将AUROC从60.85提升至63.34；在输出token数上降低计算成本4-26倍，在API调用次数上降低2.2-3.5倍；估计结果与认知不确定性的相关性大幅降低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，歧义诱发的偶然不确定性更适合从解释空间（interpretation space）而非响应空间（response space）来估计；即理解输入本身的多重解释比依赖于模型对澄清输入的答案更为有效和高效。

Abstract: A key challenge in reliable LLM deployment is recognizing when uncertainty reflects irreducible variability in the task rather than limitations in the model's knowledge. In language tasks, a central source of such aleatoric uncertainty is input ambiguity or underspecification, where multiple interpretations remain plausible. Existing decomposition methods estimate aleatoric uncertainty by generating multiple clarifications of the input, querying the model for an answer under each clarification, and comparing the resulting answers. We argue that answers are not necessary for identifying ambiguity: they are often redundant, add avoidable cost, and can mislead through epistemic leakage. We support this claim theoretically, and propose a clarification-only approach that estimates this ambiguity-induced component directly from the space of plausible interpretations, without answers to the clarified inputs. Using ambiguity detection as an operational evaluation across three benchmarks, this direct approach improves AUROC (63.34 vs. 60.85), reduces computational cost by 4-26x in output tokens and 2.2-3.5x in API calls, and yields estimates with substantially lower correlation with epistemic uncertainty. Overall, our results suggest that ambiguity-induced aleatoric uncertainty is better estimated from the interpretation space than from the response space.

</details>


### [87] [IPGeoAI: Transformer-Based Geolocation with LLM Semantic Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04559)
*Avinash Kadimisetty,Andy Jinqing Yu,Philip Favaloro,Wenlong Liu,Xiaolu Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IPGeoAI，一种基于Transformer编码器和零样本LLM特征提取的深度学习模型，将IP地理定位问题从静态查找重构为序列建模任务，在200,000个城市的专有数据集上相较领先的外部供应商在市级粒度上显著提升6%的准确率并覆盖100%流量，同时在线生产中使下游用例指标提升+0.35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于启发式规则和数据库驱动的IP地理定位方法难以解决现代网络基础设施中复杂、非线性的地址分配模式，尤其是在快速增长的IPv6地址空间和瞬态移动网络场景下表现不佳。

Method: IPGeoAI将地理定位从静态查找重构为序列建模任务：(1) 使用Transformer Encoder捕获IP子网结构中固有的层次依赖关系；(2) 通过Zero-Shot LLM Feature Extraction pipeline，利用大语言模型将原始、嘈杂的自治系统（AS）描述离线预处理为结构化的领域特定元数据（如'University' vs 'ISP'或'Global' vs 'Local'）；(3) 通过Multi-Head Cross-Attention模块将这些语义信号融合到网络中，弥合数值网络拓扑与真实世界语义身份之间的鸿沟；(4) 采用分层推理策略，先利用粗粒度国家信号再进行细化。

Result: 在覆盖200,000个城市的专有数据集上的离线评估中，IPGeoAI在市级粒度上显著优于领先的外部供应商。通过分层推理策略细化粗粒度国家信号，模型实现了6%的市级准确率提升并将覆盖率扩展到100%的流量。在大规模在线生产测试中，模型在一级下游用例指标上带来了统计显著的+0.35%提升。

Conclusion: 将深度学习序列建模与LLM驱动的语义特征提取相结合，能够有效提升IP城市级地理定位的准确性和覆盖率，为大规模互联网服务中的位置感知应用提供更可靠的支撑。

Abstract: Accurate city-level IP Geolocation is an important enabler for the modern digital ecosystem, underpinning services ranging from local content delivery and targeting to digital rights enforcement. However, traditional heuristic and database-driven methods often struggle to resolve the complex, non-linear allocation patterns of modern network infrastructures, particularly within the exploding IPv6 address space and transient mobile networks. In this paper, we introduce IPGeoAI, a novel deep learning model architecture that reframes geolocation from a static lookup problem to a sequential modeling task. Our approach utilizes the Transformer Encoder to capture hierarchical dependencies inherent in IP subnet structures. We propose a method to resolve geographic ambiguity by integrating unstructured semantic context via a Zero-Shot LLM Feature Extraction pipeline. We utilize Large Language Models to transform raw, noisy Autonomous Systems (AS) descriptions into structured, domain-specific metadata (such as 'University' vs. 'ISP' or 'Global' vs. 'Local') via an offline pre-computation process. By fusing these semantic signals into the network via a Multi-Head Cross-Attention module, we bridge the gap between numerical network topology and real-world semantic identity. Extensive offline evaluation on a proprietary dataset spanning 200,000 cities demonstrates that IPGeoAI significantly outperforms a leading external vendor in city-level granularity. By adopting a hierarchical inference strategy that refines coarse-grained country signals, our model achieves a 6% improvement in city-level accuracy while extending coverage to 100% of the traffic. Furthermore, in large-scale online production tests, the model drove a statistically significant +0.35% improvement in our 1st-tier downstream use cases metric.

</details>


### [88] [Does Your Agent's Memory Survive a Model Upgrade? A Controlled Study of Memory Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05339)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型升级时不同记忆存储机制（原始文本、RAG分块、压缩笔记、知识图谱）的迁移可靠性与修复能力，发现固定模式知识图谱迁移最稳定，压缩笔记与模型强耦合且难以修复，而保留原始历史是成功修复的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型升级是常规操作，但记忆迁移往往被忽视——新模型可能错误解读旧笔记，混合嵌入版本可能破坏检索，且缺少原始证据时修复可能失败。现有研究缺乏对模型升级后不同记忆格式可靠性的系统对比。

Method: 使用48条带随机答案编码的合成历史记录，采用精确评分，使用两个参数量小于100亿的开源模型，对比四种记忆形式在撰写者（writer）切换下的表现：LC-RAW（长上下文原文读取）、RAG（分块检索增强）、NOTES（模型压缩的自然语言笔记）、KG-fixed（固定模式知识图谱），并进行定向迁移测试和诊断分解分析。

Result: KG-fixed迁移最可靠（精度仅变化+0.0004±0.0020）；NOTES高度模型耦合，精度不对称偏移+9.91或-13.28个百分点；RAG部分嵌入迁移（50/50混合索引）仅获得4.96点提升远低于全量重嵌入的11.90点；诊断分解显示NOTES精度缺陷的80%源于初始信息损失，RAG缺陷的81%由检索失败驱动；NOTES仅存储修复在48个测试用例中全部未达90%恢复目标，而保留原始历史的修复可在48例中的34例成功恢复。

Conclusion: 研究强调模型迁移需要方向特异性测试、严格的嵌入空间隔离以及保留源历史以支持记忆修复。

Abstract: Model upgrades are routine; memory migrations are not. An agent can keep the same memory store and still forget: a new model may interpret old notes differently, mixed embedding versions may break retrieval, and repair may fail without the original evidence. We compare memory as the same history is preserved verbatim for long-context reading (LC-RAW), divided into chunks for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), compressed by a model into natural-language notes (NOTES), or normalized into a fixed-schema knowledge graph (KG-fixed). The study uses 48 synthetic histories with randomized answer codes, exact scoring, and two open-weight models with sub 10 billion parameters.
  Our measurements show that fixed-schema structures transfer reliably, with KG-fixed accuracy changing by only $+0.0004 \pm 0.0020$ following a writer swap. Conversely, compressed NOTES exhibit high model coupling, with accuracy shifting asymmetrically by $+9.91$ or $-13.28$ percentage points depending on the specific migration direction. In RAG systems, partial embedding migrations using a 50/50 mixed index capture only a 4.96-point accuracy improvement, forfeiting the majority of the 11.90-point gain achieved through full re-embedding. Diagnostic decomposition attributes 80% ($0.467 \pm 0.014$) of the NOTES accuracy deficit to information lost during initial construction, whereas retrieval failures drive 81% ($0.364 \pm 0.012$) of the RAG deficit. Finally, store-only repair of NOTES fails to reach a 90% performance recovery target in all 48 test cases, whereas retaining the raw source history enables successful recovery in 34 of 48 cases for one tested direction. These findings highlight the necessity of direction-specific migration testing, strict embedding space isolation, and the retention of source histories for memory repair.

</details>


### [89] [Reducing Hallucinated Transcripts in Whisper via Hallucination Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04561)
*Maryam Abbasihafshejani,Murtuza Jadliwala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练、仅推理时的低秩投影方法，将Whisper解码器激活投影远离幻觉关联子空间，在非语音基准上将幻觉率从31.31%降至2.44%（92.21%相对降低），同时通过门控变体在抑制幻觉与保持语音识别性能之间提供可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper作为广泛使用的ASR基础模型，其生成式解码器在输入包含少量或无语音时会产生流畅的幻觉转录，影响实际应用的可信度。现有方法需要重新训练或修改模型结构，效率低下且成本高昂。

Method: 提出一种训练无关的推理时方法：从非语音校准数据中估计一个低秩的幻觉关联子空间，然后在推理时将解码器隐藏状态投影远离该子空间。设计了两种变体：always-on（对所有输入施加投影）和gated（仅在Whisper预测输入可能为非语音时施加投影）。

Result: 在非语音基准上，always-on投影将平均幻觉率从31.31%降至2.44%（相对降低92.21%）；gated投影将幻觉率降至3.74%（相对降低88.05%）。在LibriSpeech上，gated投影使绝对WER增加0.33-4.39个百分点，FRR为0.41%-9.97%。

Conclusion: 低秩激活投影能够在无需重新训练的情况下大幅抑制Whisper的幻觉现象，同时在幻觉抑制与语音识别性能之间提供了可控的权衡，为后续相关方法提供了有用参考。

Abstract: Whisper is a widely used foundation model for automatic speech recognition (ASR), but its generative decoder can produce fluent hallucinated transcripts for inputs containing little or no speech. We propose a training-free, inference-time method to reduce these hallucinations using low-rank projection of decoder activations. A compact hallucination-associated subspace is estimated from non-speech calibration data, and decoder hidden states are projected away from this subspace during inference.
  We evaluate two variants: always-on, which applies projection to all inputs, and gated, which applies it only when Whisper predicts that an input is likely non-speech. Across non-speech benchmarks, always-on projection reduces average hallucination rate (HR) from 31.31% to 2.44%, a 92.21% relative reduction, while gated projection reduces HR to 3.74%, an 88.05% relative reduction, with lower false rejection of genuine speech. On LibriSpeech, gated projection increases absolute word error rate (WER) by 0.33-4.39 percentage points and yields false-rejection rates (FRR) of 0.41--9.97% across model and split settings.
  These results show that low-rank activation projection can substantially suppress Whisper hallucinations without retraining, while providing a controllable trade-off between hallucination suppression and speech recognition performance.

</details>


### [90] [Extremely Sparse Supervision Incentivizes Reasoning Ability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04565)
*Zhishuai Liu,Xingzi Xu,Mehmet Saygin Seyfioglu,Pan Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现一个反直觉现象：在on-policy蒸馏（OPD）设置下，仅用每条推理轨迹中1-2个token（约占全部token的0.05%）的极稀疏监督，就能在提升推理能力上媲美甚至超越全token训练。该发现在9种教师-学生配置、数学与代码推理、Llama模型以及基于PPO的RLVR中均得到验证，挑战了"有效后训练必须依赖token密集型学习"的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法通常在大规模token上进行优化，隐含假设有效学习必须是token密集型的。作者希望重新审视这一假设，探究在密集教师监督可得的OPD设置下，推理能力是否真的需要如此大量的token监督。

Method: 在on-policy distillation（OPD）设置下，基于Qwen3模型家族，仅从每条推理轨迹中选取极少量的token（1-2个）构造训练目标，排除绝大多数生成的token，将其与全token训练在推理能力上进行系统性对比。实验覆盖9种教师-学生配置、不同模型规模、数学与代码推理任务，并扩展验证到Llama模型和PPO-based RLVR。

Result: 极稀疏监督（每条轨迹仅1-2个token，约0.05%）在大多数情况下匹配或超越全token训练对推理能力的提升；该现象在9种教师-学生配置、数学和代码推理任务、Llama模型以及PPO-based RLVR中均一致观察得到。

Conclusion: 有效的后训练并不一定需要token密集型的监督。极稀疏的关键token监督可能更接近自然的反思式学习过程（关注少数关键推理步骤而非逐字纠错），为设计和理解更高效的后训练算法提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models demonstrate increasingly strong reasoning capabilities through effective post-training. Yet, prevailing post-training methods optimize over massive numbers of tokens, implicitly assuming that effective learning must be token-intensive. We revisit this assumption in the on-policy distillation (OPD) setting, which naturally admits dense teacher supervision at every generated token. Using the Qwen3 family, we discover a counter-intuitive phenomenon: reasoning can be effectively incentivized by an extremely small fraction of generated tokens--as few as one or two tokens per reasoning trajectory, corresponding to only 0.05% of all tokens. Surprisingly, this sparse supervision in most cases matches or surpasses full-token training in improving reasoning ability, despite excluding the vast majority of generated tokens from the training objective. This phenomenon is consistently observed across nine teacher--student configurations spanning different model scales on mathematical reasoning tasks, and is further validated on coding reasoning, Llama models and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based reinforcement learning with verifiable reward (RLVR). Interestingly, such extremely sparse supervision may be closer to the natural learning process: rather than correcting every step word by word, one reflects on a few critical reasoning steps, updates prior understanding, and continues the trial-and-error, avoiding micro-level corrections while remaining remarkably effective. Overall, our results challenge the assumption that effective post-training must be token-intensive and point to a new direction for understanding and designing more efficient post-training algorithms.

</details>


### [91] [Does the Selected Object Reach the Reader? Auditing Identity Handoffs in Grounded Language-Model Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04579)
*Siddharth Vohra,Runmin Jiang,Xiaomo Li,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了HybridQA中"选定的对象"与"数据集追踪的段落"两者身份不一致的问题，发现仅凭对象选择无法保证检出对应证据段落，提出了Returned-Object Profile (ROP)作为可复现的执行记录与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接地语言模型管道分为选择对象、检索段落、基于证据回答三个阶段。如果选定的对象必须到达读者（即下游检索/回答依赖该对象），丢失对象会破坏信息交接。然而基准评测（benchmark recall）检查的是数据集链接的对象，与被选中的对象可能不同，这种身份差异可能导致对系统真实能力的误判。

Method: 作者审计了600个HybridQA问题，覆盖三种选择器（selector）家族。将选定对象与数据集追踪的段落进行身份比对，比较精确键查找、精确标题匹配、仅正文BM25（cutoff=5）以及带重排序的混合检索等不同检索规则下对象的找回率，并通过冻结阅读器（frozen reader）对比及64项特意选取的消融队列验证段落的因果作用。

Result: 在1,463条选定对象与数据集追踪段落匹配的可解析记录中，精确键查找与标题匹配总是能找回对象；但同一解码标题下，body-only BM25在389条（26.6%）记录上于cutoff=5时遗漏对象，而带重排序的混合检索仅在14条（1.0%）遗漏。两种身份（选定对象 vs 数据集追踪段落）在1,792条可解析记录中的329条（18.4%）上不一致；基于原始问题的排序下，两者的top-5检出在106条（5.9%）上分歧。冻结阅读器比对显示对齐对象的存在可带来28.6至31.0个精确匹配（EM）点的提升；在64项消融队列中，移除该段落大幅降低EM，而移除等长对照段落不会复现此降幅。

Conclusion: 基准中的段落追踪身份与待检对象身份常存在实质分歧，评估应显式追踪"返回对象是否到达读者"。作者发布Returned-Object Profile (ROP)，一种可执行记录，包含目标对象、返回的ID字段、cutoff、成员规则与完整预期总体，附带数据与离线重放以便复现和扩展审计。

Abstract: Grounded language-model pipelines can be divided into three stages: selecting an object, retrieving passages for it, and using that evidence to answer. If the selected object must reach the reader, losing it breaks the handoff. Benchmark recall checks the dataset-linked object, which can differ. We audit 600 HybridQA questions across three selector families. On 1,463 resolvable records where the selected object matches the dataset-traced passage, exact key lookup and exact title matching return the object every time. With every ranked rule given the same decoded selected title, body-only BM25 omits it on 389 records (26.6%) at cutoff five, while hybrid retrieval with reranking omits it on 14 (1.0%). The two identities differ on 329 of 1,792 resolvable records. With original-question rankings, their top-five checks disagree on 106 records (5.9%). Frozen reader comparisons associate the aligned object's presence with 28.6 to 31.0 points higher exact match. In a deliberately selected 64-item cohort, removing that passage sharply lowers exact match, while removing a similar-length comparison passage does not reproduce the drop. We release the Returned-Object Profile (ROP), an executable record of the target, returned-ID field, cutoff, membership rule, and complete expected population, with data and an offline replay.

</details>


### [92] [$τ^τ$-Bench: An Environment for End-To-End, Realistic Agent Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04611)
*Quan Shi,Keshav Dhandhania,Karthik Narasimhan,Victor Barres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ττ-bench基准测试，将构建agent本身作为评测任务。开发者agent需要基于真实业务记录、客户需求、生产API、遗留代码库以及服务成本和模型限制，交付完整的客服agent，并通过部署该agent对抗留出模拟用户来打分。在53个跨四领域的任务中，最强配置（Claude Code下的Claude Opus 5）仅通过23.9%的评测模拟，而专家参考实现达到82.2%。失败模式与人类agent开发者遇到的类似：模型只发浅层查询而非深度理解记录、与客户沟通极少、对agent架构和服务开支实验不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agents正在成为生产软件，而构建这些agent的工作越来越多地由编码agent承担。然而现有基准很少检验AI系统能否在真实客户接洽条件下交付一个完整的agent，因此需要一个以agent构建本身为任务的评测基准。

Method: 研究者设计了ττ-bench，为开发者agent提供真实业务保留的记录、持有需求的客户、必须经由的生产API、需继承的代码库，以及对服务成本和模型的限制——还原真实接洽的起点。agent需交付完整客服agent，其评分为将该agent部署对抗留出模拟用户。

Result: 在53个跨四个领域的任务中，最强配置Claude Opus 5在Claude Code下仅通过23.9%的评测模拟，而专家编写参考实现得分82.2%。失败模式包括浅层查询取代深度记录理解、与客户沟通极少、对架构与服务开支实验不足。

Conclusion: ττ-bench旨在将协作式agent构建工作转化为编码agent可衡量的目标，揭示当前最强AI系统在真实客户环境中交付agent的能力仍远不及专家水平。

Abstract: LLM agents are rapidly becoming production software, deployed to handle customer service, adjudicate disputes, and operate internal systems. Notably, the work of building them is increasingly handed to coding agents, yet existing benchmarks say little about whether an AI system can deliver one under the conditions of a real client engagement. We introduce $τ^τ$-bench (pronounced hyper-tau-bench), a benchmark that makes agent construction the task. A developer agent is given the records a business actually keeps, a client who holds requirements, a production API that operations must run through, a codebase to inherit, and limits on serving cost and models: the same starting point a real engagement provides. From these it must deliver a complete customer-service agent, scored by deploying that agent against held-out simulated users. Across 53 tasks spanning four domains, the strongest configuration, Claude Opus 5 under Claude Code, passes just 23.9% of evaluation simulations. Meanwhile, an expert-authored reference ceiling scores 82.2%. The failures mirror ones human agent developers see: models issue shallow queries in place of deep comprehension of the records, communicate almost nothing to the client, and experiment too little with agent architecture and serving spend, shipping the first design that runs. We aim for $τ^τ$-bench to turn the work of cooperative agent building into a measurable target for coding agents.

</details>


### [93] [Leveraging Imperfect Restoration for Data Availability Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04627)
*Yi Huang,Jeremy Styborski,Mingzhi Lyu,Fan Wang,Adams Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Imperfect Restoration Poisoning (IRP)的新型数据投毒方法，用于对抗深度学习模型对在线数据的未经授权训练。IRP在保持高图像质量的同时，实现了对监督学习和自监督学习场景下均有效且强健的投毒效果，克服了现有CUDA方法在自监督学习下效果不佳及图像质量与投毒效果之间难以兼顾的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线数据面临被未经授权用于训练深度学习模型的风险，现有数据可用性攻击(DAAs)通常在监督学习(SL)或自监督学习(SSL)场景中各有侧重。虽然基于卷积的无学习数据集(CUDA)是目前对SL和SSL均最强的模型无关攻击方法，但其在SSL下的有效性不足，且面临图像质量与投毒效果之间的严重权衡。因此需要一种既能保持高图像质量又能实现强投毒效果的新方法。

Method: 作者首先对CUDA进行了理论分析，揭示了其引入的次优梯度以及通过诱导类别偏差进行数据投毒的策略。在此基础上，提出名为Imperfect Restoration Poisoning (IRP)的新型投毒方法，旨在同时实现高图像质量和强投毒效果。

Result: IRP在SL和SSL场景下与八种基线方法进行了广泛比较，并联合五种代表性防御方法进行了评估，结果表明IRP具备显著优越性。

Conclusion: IRP是对CUDA的改进与超越，能够在不牺牲图像质量的前提下保持强大的数据投毒效果，在SL和SSL两大场景中均表现优异，是一种更具实用性的数据可用性攻击方法。

Abstract: The abundance of online data is at risk of unauthorized usage in training deep learning models. To counter this, various Data Availability Attacks (DAAs) have been devised to make data unlearnable for such models by subtly perturbing the training data. However, existing attacks often excel against either Supervised Learning (SL) or Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) scenarios. Among these, a model-free approach that generates a Convolution-based Unlearnable Dataset (CUDA) stands out as the most robust DAA across both SSL and SL. Nonetheless, CUDA's effectiveness against SSL is underwhelming and it faces a severe trade-off between image quality and its poisoning effect. In this paper, we conduct a theoretical analysis of CUDA, uncovering the sub-optimal gradients it introduces and elucidating the strategy it employs to induce class-wise bias for data poisoning. Building on this, we propose a novel poisoning method named Imperfect Restoration Poisoning (IRP), aiming to preserve high image quality while achieving strong poisoning effects. Through extensive comparisons of IRP with eight baselines across SL and SSL, coupled with evaluations alongside five representative defense methods, we showcase the superiority of IRP. Code: https://github.com/lyumingzhi/IRP

</details>


### [94] [SiLR: Structure-Preserving Admission and Process Reward for LLM Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04629)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM工具智能体运行门控（runtime gate）的问题。作者指出传统门控基于聚合标量分数（如violation count）设计存在"标量投影陷阱"（scalar projection trap），会接受局部改进的提议却使轨迹陷入平台期。提出SiLR方法，通过影子执行（shadow-execution）每个提议，并用分支级违规状态上的积序（product order，涵盖过载分支支持和各分支严重度）来决定准入。作者证明了不存在对积序sound的标量替代，因此失败是表征性的而非阈值调节问题。在Gym-ANM基准上SiLR在21个多动作场景中全部恢复（21/21），远超终态门控的0/21和最优标量门的9/21。该结论在三种模型家族和CityLearn上保持一致。此外，由于准入基于确定性模拟，LLM位于信任边界之外；面对幅度重分配攻击（magnitude-redistribution attack），仅完整的分支级谓词才能防御。当两个约束族同时激活时，所有标量投影都会准入物理不安全的动作。用作GRPO过程奖励时，SiLR在所有挖掘场景中优于计数投影，且是唯一在未门控策略上超过未训练基线的被测奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工具智能体运行门控通常被设计为过滤器，在一个违规建议被拒绝后同一状态会提出新建议，因此门控本质上承担着搜索算子的功能。现有的聚合评分门控（scalar projection gate）会落入标量投影陷阱：接受局部改进提议导致轨迹陷入平台期，无法有效恢复。作者试图解决多分支违规场景下门控的结构性表征缺陷，揭示标量表征从根本上无法sound地反映违规几何结构。

Method: 提出SiLR方法：对每个LLM提议进行影子执行（shadow-execution），利用确定性模拟评估各分支的违规状态（包括过载分支数量和逐分支违规严重度），然后依据积序（product order）判定是否准入该提议。该准入准则不依赖LLM自身的判断，将LLM置于信任边界之外。同时理论证明了不存在任何标量替代（surrogate）对积序是sound的，说明该失败源于表征维度的本质缺失而非阈值调节。

Result: 在Gym-ANM挖掘出的24个场景上：SiLR恢复21/21多动作情节，终态门控为0/21，最佳标量门为9/21。终态vs结构化二分法在三种模型家族和CityLearn中保持一致。面对幅度重分配攻击，标量和仅支持（support-only）基线均被攻破，仅完整分支级谓词（product order）能防御。在两约束族同时激活时，所有标量投影均准入物理不安全动作，support-only准入比例最高（42,410个中的63.2%），积序为0。在双族最困难轨迹上，标量门仅可通过不安全动作类恢复。作为GRPO过程奖励复用，SiLR在所有挖掘场景中优于其计数投影，是唯一门控后策略超过未训练基线的奖励（0.844 vs 0.778）。

Conclusion: 本文从表征层面论证了运行门控中聚合标量分数的根本局限：违规几何是结构性的、多分支的，无法被压缩为sound的标量投影。只有包含逐分支违规严重度和过载分支支持度的完整积序谓词才能在结构上充分支撑安全且有效的恢复行为。这一发现在恢复成功率、安全约束满足、对抗攻击（幅度重分配）鲁棒性以及GRPO奖励学习等多个设计点上得到一致验证。

Abstract: A runtime gate for an LLM tool agent is usually cast as a filter. In a ReAct loop a rejected proposal is followed by another at the same state, so the gate is a search operator over the proposal stream whose admission criterion shapes which trajectories are reachable. We study post-violation recovery admission, where progress must be admitted while the system is still in violation, and identify the scalar projection trap: an aggregate-score gate accepts a locally improving proposal and commits the trajectory to a plateau. SiLR instead shadow-executes each proposal and admits it under a product order over the branch-level violation state (overloaded-branch support and per-branch severity). We prove that no scalar surrogate is sound for this order, so the failure is representational, not a matter of threshold tuning. On mined Gym-ANM scenarios, SiLR recovers 21/21 multi-action episodes against 0/21 for terminal and 9/21 for the best scalar gate, significant across the full 24-scenario benchmark. The terminal-versus-structured dichotomy holds across three model families and in CityLearn. Because admission rests on deterministic simulation, the LLM lies outside the trust boundary: a magnitude-redistribution attack that defeats both scalar and support-only baselines is contained only by the full per-branch predicate. With two constraint families active, every tested scalar projection admits physically unsafe actions; support-only admits the largest fraction (63.2% of 42,410; product order 0). In the hardest dual-family traces, scalar gates recover only through that unsafe class. Reused as a GRPO process reward, it outperforms its count projection in every mined scenario and is the only tested reward whose ungated policy exceeds the untrained base (0.844 vs. 0.778). Scalar projection loses the violation geometry at both design points; only the full product order is structurally sufficient.

</details>


### [95] [A Cost-Aware Agentic Architecture for NL-to-SQL over Nested Enterprise Schemas, with a New Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04641)
*Yoga Sri Varshan Varadharajan,Ajay Yadav,Ritesh Goru,Prateek Chaudhury,Constantine Caramanis,Prateek Jain,Divyateja Pasupuleti,Sunil Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DevRev NL2SQL基准（含900条执行验证的查询与Semantic Depth Score评估框架），并介绍一种成本感知的单次生成智能体架构，该架构针对企业级图状/半结构化/深层嵌套的schema设计，在基准上达到91.7%准确率，显著优于基线，并在Spider 2.0上达到竞争水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言转SQL系统在学术基准上表现优异，但企业生产环境的schema具有图状、半结构化、深层嵌套等特性，现有基准无法衡量这些能力，缺乏针对该场景的评估体系与解决方案。

Method: 作者做了两项互补贡献：(1) 提出DevRev NL2SQL基准，包含900条执行验证的查询，涵盖嵌套类型与链接图结构，并配套Schema无关的Semantic Depth Score（SDS）评分体系衡量分析推理深度；(2) 提出成本感知的单次生成智能体架构，其schema选择、元数据检索与错误修复等组件均针对该场景需求设计。

Result: 在DevRev NL2SQL基准上达到91.7%的答案正确率，比次优基线高出54.6个百分点；在Spider 2.0 Snowflake公开数据集上，单次生成运行点下与领先系统具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了企业级复杂schema场景下NL2SQL评估基准的空白，验证了针对该场景设计的成本感知单次生成架构的高效性与有效性，为复杂真实世界NL2SQL任务提供了新的评价标准与实用方案。

Abstract: Natural-language-to-SQL systems have ad- vanced rapidly on academic benchmarks, yet production enterprise schemas exhibit graph- like, semi-structured, deeply nested structure that current benchmarks do not measure. We make two complementary contributions. First, we introduce the DevRev NL2SQL bench- mark: 900 execution-verified queries with nested-type and link-graph structure, accom- panied by the Semantic Depth Score (SDS), a schema-agnostic rubric for analytical reasoning depth. Second, we present a cost-aware single- generation agentic architecture whose schema- selection, metadata-retrieval, and error-repair components are designed for the requirements this regime imposes. On the DevRev NL2SQL benchmark the system attains 91.7% answer correctness, a margin of 54.6 percentage points over the next-best baseline; on the Spider 2.0 Snowflake public dataset, it is competitive with leading systems at a single-generation operating point.

</details>


### [96] [Continual Graph Memory for Adaptive Recommendation under Intent Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04651)
*Hao Nguyen Ngoc,Tung Nguyen,Nguyen Thi Hanh,Hoang Thai Dinh,Nguyen Xuan Tung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CGM-Rec，一种面向意图漂移下自适应推荐的持续图记忆框架。它将知识图谱视为可写记忆，包含语义图记忆（质量门控的保守更新，存储稳定关系知识）和情景教训记忆（快速反应记忆，学习近期结果与失败案例）。测试阶段模型参数冻结，仅通过记忆写入实现适应。实验表明在多种推荐设置下优于神经与LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统KG增强推荐系统将图视为静态检索底物，难以应对不断演变的用户意图、噪声元数据和重复出现的失败模式。推荐过程中反馈可能揭示排序所用关系证据是有用、缺失还是误导性的，因此需要一种能够动态适应意图漂移的框架。

Method: CGM-Rec将图状态视为可写记忆，包含两个互补组件：(1) Semantic Graph Memory（语义图记忆）通过质量门控的类型化操作保守更新，用于存储稳定且高置信度的关系知识；(2) Episodic Lesson Memory（情景教训记忆）作为快速反应记忆，学习近期结果、失败案例和纠正性提示。测试时模型参数冻结，适应仅通过记忆写入完成。

Result: 在冻结参数、单遍重排协议下，CGM-Rec在多种推荐设置中大部分指标优于神经与LLM基线。在采样候选重排下，Bundle数据集HR@1较最强LLM基线提升最高达29.58%；在元数据丰富的ML-100K上，HR@5达到0.5941，优于K-RagRec的0.4746。

Conclusion: CGM-Rec通过将知识图谱构建为可写的持续记忆结构，有效处理意图漂移情境下的自适应推荐问题。双记忆设计在保持稳定语义知识的同时快速学习近期经验，且测试阶段无需微调参数即可实现适应，验证了该范式在已知与LLM基线竞争中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies adaptive recommendation under intent drift, where feedback from each recommendation outcome can reveal whether the relational evidence used for ranking is useful, missing, or misleading. While Knowledge Graphs (KGs) provide essential semantic structure to handle these shifts, traditional KG-enhanced systems treat the graph as a static retrieval substrate, making it brittle to evolving intents, noisy metadata, and recurring failure patterns. This paper proposes CGM-Rec, a continual graph memory framework for adaptive recommendation. CGM-Rec treats the graph state as a writable memory and maintains two complementary components. Therein, a Semantic Graph Memory is updated conservatively through quality-gated typed operations for storing stable and high-confidence relational knowledge. Meanwhile, an Episodic Lesson Memory acts as a fast reactive memory that learns recent outcomes, failure cases, and corrective hints. During testing, model parameters remain frozen and adaptation occurs only through memory writes. We evaluate CGM-Rec under a frozen-parameter, one-pass reranking protocol, where encoders and prompts remain fixed during testing and adaptation occurs only through memory writes. Experiments across multiple recommendation settings show that CGM-Rec improves over evaluated neural and LLM-based baselines on most metrics. Particularly, under sampled-candidate reranking, CGM-Rec improves HR@1 by up to 29.58% over the strongest LLM baseline on Bundle, and outperforms K-RagRec on metadata-rich ML-100K with HR@5 of 0.5941 versus 0.4746.

</details>


### [97] [Harness-agnostic detection and immunization of reward hacking in self-evolving language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04665)
*Rongxin Yang,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Haoxuan Jia,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Yingguang Yang,Yulin Huang,Jianshen Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HackProbe，一种通过两个黑盒钩子附着于任意自我进化循环的监控与免疫系统，用于检测并缓解奖励欺骗（reward hacking）。它通过保持分布固定的秘密比较核心与轮换新层来抵御共同适应，构建四个测试并施以Sidak校正生成校准的族级p值；在检测到攻击后通过风险感知免疫层从候选池中重新选取诚实候选。实验表明其AUROC达0.763，优于最强基线0.663，并将假阳性率从0.706降至0.434。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化语言模型通过提出候选更新并保留能提升可见分数的更新来改进自身。当该分数是所期望能力的不完美代理时，持续选择会扩大两者差距，即奖励欺骗。现有方法缺乏对黑盒、无权重/激活访问场景下奖励欺骗的通用检测与免疫机制。

Method: HackProbe包含：(1) 秘密、分布固定的比较核心，使能力代理跨代可比；(2) 轮换的新鲜层，硬化候选库抵御共同适应；(3) 基于核心的四个测试：水平差距、带在线变点检测的尺度对齐散度、能力停滞、条件自信-错误率，经Sidak校正形成族级p值；(4) 风险感知免疫层，用核心和纯结构性作弊足迹从提案池重新选择诚实候选，每代至多向宿主披露log2 Pi比特。还证明了可检测性界（将目标错误率转为显式探针规模预算）并界定探针轮换的收益边界。

Result: 在带四种注入作弊通道及真值标签的受控prompt级宿主上，HackProbe达到0.763 AUROC（最强基线0.663），假阳性率从0.706降至0.434。带宽受限的重新选择是唯一的免疫级别：在作弊下平均多返回5.2点真实能力，在干净运行中仅损失4.7点；各通道效果大多不具单独显著性。

Conclusion: HackProbe证明可以在无权重/激活访问的黑盒条件下有效检测奖励欺骗，并通过带宽受限的诚实候选再选择在作弊场景下净增益真实能力，尽管单通道免疫效果显著性有限，整体方案展现了检测与免疫兼顾的可行设计空间。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models improve by proposing candidate updates and keeping whatever raises a visible score. When that score is an imperfect proxy for the capability one actually wants, sustained selection widens the gap between the two. This is reward hacking. We introduce HackProbe, a monitor that attaches to an arbitrary self-evolving loop through two black-box hooks, with no access to weights or activations. It keeps a secret, distribution-fixed comparison core, whose frozen distribution makes its capability proxy comparable across generations, alongside a rotated fresh layer that hardens the bank against co-adaptation. Four tests built on that proxy cover the level gap, a scale-aligned divergence with online change-point detection, capability stagnation, and a conditional confidently-wrong rate; a Sidak correction turns them into a calibrated family-wise p-value. Diagnosis alone recovers nothing, so a risk-aware immunization layer reselects an honest candidate from the proposal pool using the core together with a purely structural gaming footprint, disclosing at most log2 Pi bits per generation to the host. We prove a detectability bound that converts a target error rate into an explicit probe-size budget, and we delimit what probe rotation does and does not buy. On a controlled prompt-level host with four injected hacking channels and ground-truth labels, HackProbe reaches 0.763 AUROC against 0.663 for the strongest baseline and cuts the false-positive rate from 0.706 to 0.434. Its bandwidth-limited reselection is the only immunization level that returns more true capability under hacking, 5.2 points on average, than it forfeits on clean runs, 4.7; per-channel effects are mostly not individually significant.

</details>


### [98] [ERPBench: Evaluating LLM Agents for Enterprise Decision-Making Across Competitive Market Ecologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04667)
*Xinran Zhang,Pengrui Lu,Lyumanshan Ye,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ERPBench是一个执行插桩的企业决策智能体基准测试，在六轮ERP模拟中结合定价、生产、采购、库存、财务和共享市场竞争，将相同的100个固定问题置于两种匹配的竞争性市场生态中评估：Solo（单个LLM智能体对抗固定规则对手）和Arena（六个LLM智能体在共享市场中竞争）。实验表明不同生态下领先模型不同，两个生态在100个问题上仅21个识别出相同的任务级赢家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体评估很少检验企业决策结论是否能跨竞争性市场生态迁移，而企业工作流中的决策结论可能因市场生态不同而失效，需要一个支持配对评估的基准来系统检验这一问题。

Method: 构建ERPBench基准：(1) 六轮ERP仿真模拟，耦合定价、生产、采购、库存、财务和共享市场竞争；(2) 在Solo（对固定规则对手）和Arena（六个模型在共享市场彼此竞争）两种匹配生态中评估相同的100个固定问题；(3) 覆盖六个模型家族，产生1200条模型级轨迹、7200个决策回合；(4) 辅以聚合并行执行干预分析。

Result: 在观测服务配置下，不同生态中的领先模型不同：DeepSeek在Solo中领先（252.29M平均估值，平均排名1.67），而Gemini在Arena中领先（263.95M，1.76）。两种生态仅在100个问题中的21个上识别出相同的任务级赢家，且Gemini的末位比例从Arena中的22%降至0%。

Conclusion: ERPBench揭示了企业智能体排名在不同竞争性市场生态间缺乏稳定性，说明评估结论可能不随单一市场条件迁移，支持配对评估和干预分析以验证企业决策智能体的跨生态泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed for enterprise workflows, yet existing evaluations rarely test whether business-decision conclusions transfer across competitive market ecologies. We introduce ERPBench, an execution-instrumented benchmark for enterprise decision agents in a six-round Enterprise Resource Planning (ERP) simulation with coupled pricing, production, procurement, inventory, finance, and shared-market competition. ERPBench evaluates the same 100 fixed problems in two matched competitive market ecologies: Solo, where each evaluated LLM agent competes against fixed rule-based opponents, and Arena, where six evaluated LLM agents compete in a shared market. Across six model families, this yields 1,200 model-level trajectories spanning 7,200 decision rounds. Under the observed service configuration, the leading model differs between ecologies: DeepSeek leads in Solo (252.29M mean valuation; mean rank 1.67), whereas Gemini leads in Arena (263.95M; 1.76). The two ecologies identify the same task-level winner on only 21 of 100 problems, and Gemini's bottom-rank rate falls from 22 % to 0 % in Arena. ERPBench supports paired evaluation of whether enterprise-agent rankings transfer across competitive market ecologies, supplemented by aggregate execution-intervention analysis. Code and benchmark resources are available in our https://github.com/GAIR-NLP/erp-bench.

</details>


### [99] [Train What You Deploy:Token-Faithful Post-Training of a Production Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04678)
*Cheng Li,Jiexiong Liu,Yixuan Chen,Chi Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种保真感知的训练耦合框架（C-DPPO），解决编码与终端代理后训练管线中token与控制保真度错误问题，通过协商训练协议消除虚假调用，将损失计算限制在可验证的token跨度内，并在Baize5B/Baize10B模型上相较标准DPPO获得一致的+3.0点性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码与终端代理的后训练管线存在严重的token和控制保真度错误：简化的训练环境与生产部署不匹配，且从agent日志进行离线token重建会扭曲原始提示、混淆策略调用与后台模型操作，导致训练信号失真。

Method: 提出保真感知的训练耦合框架：在训练侧保留对原始提示的采样能力；通过协商训练协议消除虚假模型调用；将损失计算限制在具有闭合失败保证的可验证token跨度上。在此基础上提出C-DPPO，在标准DPPO之上建立紧致的两侧TV认证界、自适应K规则、预算感知序列保证及误差鲁棒性策略掩码。

Result: 在TMax-100上以相同的训练与测试协议对匹配的Baize5B和Baize10B模型进行评估，C-DPPO相比标准DPPO在各模型规模上取得一致的+3.0点性能提升。证书审计验证了所提认证训练管线的可靠性与完整运行覆盖率。

Conclusion: C-DPPO通过系统的保真度修复与认证机制，在无需额外模型规模增益的情况下稳定提升训练效果，且其证书审计机制保证了管线在实际运行中的可靠性与充分覆盖。

Abstract: Existing post-training pipelines for coding and terminal agents suffer severe token and control fidelity errors: simplified training environments mismatch production deployments, and offline token reconstruction from agent logs distorts original prompts and conflates policy calls with background model operations. We present a fidelity-aware training coupling framework that retains trainer-side sampling over original prompts, eliminates spurious model calls via a negotiated training protocol, and restricts loss computation to verifiable token spans with closed-failure guarantees. We further propose Certified Divergence Proximal Policy Optimization (C-DPPO), which establishes tight two-sided TV certification bounds, adaptive-K rules, budget-aware sequence guarantees, and error-robust policy masking atop standard DPPO. Evaluated on matched Baize5B and Baize10B models with identical training and test protocols on TMax-100, C-DPPO yields a consistent +3.0-point performance gain over standard DPPO across model scales. Certificate audits validate the reliability and full operational coverage of our certified training pipeline.

</details>


### [100] [Predicting Spatiotemporal Mobile Sensing-Based PM2.5 Concentrations Using Low-Rank Adapted Spatially Attentive Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04693)
*Om Chiddarwar,Priyanka Mandal,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya,Shriniwas Arkatkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个来自印度苏拉特的城市移动传感PM2.5数据集，并提出SA-GNN（Space-Aware Graph Neural Network）模型进行细粒度短期PM2.5预测与热点识别，该混合架构结合聚类专属GRU捕获局部时间依赖和图注意力网络学习空间异质性，在R²=0.95、RMSE=6.8、MAE=4.2 µg/m³的性能上优于LSTM、RNN、GRU和ANN等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空气质量沿交通走廊存在显著时空变化，需要进行高分辨率监测。现有模型（如LSTM、RNN等）在低分辨率数据上表现良好，但难以捕捉城市空气质量快速变化的时空模式，亟需能处理细粒度、高维移动传感数据并建模空间异质性与局部时间依赖的模型。

Method: 构建了包含PM2.5浓度、气象变量（温度、湿度、风速、风向）及土地利用特征的移动传感数据集。采用两种节点定义策略将时空数据表示为图：(i)均匀分段（200-400米间隔）和(ii)DBSCAN聚类自适应分组密集观测。每个节点计算气象变量的滚动均值和标准差。提出SA-GNN模型，使用聚类专属GRU捕获局部时间依赖，使用图注意力网络学习空间异质性。

Result: 在苏拉特数据集上SA-GNN达到R²=0.95，RMSE=6.8，MAE=4.2 µg/m³，性能优于所有基线模型（LSTM、RNN、GRU、ANN），这些基线虽在低分辨率数据上表现良好但难以捕捉城市空气质量的快速变化模式。

Conclusion: 结合空间聚类与自适应注意力的混合图神经网络能显著改善空气质量预测，支持实时细粒度监测、个性化暴露追踪和及时警报，有助于建设更健康的城市。

Abstract: Urban air quality can vary significantly along transit corridors, necessitating high-resolution monitoring. This work introduces a novel mobile-sensing dataset from Surat, Gujarat, India, comprising PM$*{2.5}$ concentrations, meteorological variables (temperature, humidity, wind speed, wind direction), and land-use features. To represent the spatiotemporal data as a graph, two node-definition strategies were used: (i) uniform segmentation (200--400~m intervals) and (ii) DBSCAN clustering to adaptively group dense observations. For each node, rolling mean and standard deviation of meteorological variables were computed. To model this high-dimensional data, we propose a SA-GNN for fine-grained, short-term PM$*{2.5}$ forecasting and hotspot identification. We compared SA-GNN with LSTM, RNN, GRU, and ANN models. These models performed well on low-resolution data but had difficulty capturing rapidly changing patterns in urban air quality. SA-GNN employs cluster-specific GRUs to capture localized temporal dependencies and a Graph Attention Network to learn spatial heterogeneity. This hybrid architecture effectively models rapid fluctuations and complex spatial interactions. On our dataset, SA-GNN achieved $R^2 = 0.95$, RMSE $= 6.8$, and MAE $= 4.2~\si{\micro\gram\per\meter\cubed}$, outperforming all baseline models. Combining spatial clustering with adaptive attention significantly improves forecasting, enabling real-time, fine-grained monitoring and supporting personalized exposure tracking and timely alerts for healthier cities.

</details>


### [101] [SQL-Zero: Self-Evolving Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04697)
*Daniel Machado Pedrozo,Julia Soares Dollis,Bryan Lincoln Marques de Oliveira,Vinicius Alboneti Aguiar,Sávio Salvarino Teles de Oliveira,Telma Woerle de Lima Soares*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SQL-Zero，一种无需人工标注自然语言/SQL对即可训练的Text-to-SQL求解器。通过proposer-solver自我对弈（self-play），挑战者与求解器基于同一基座LLM，唯一监督信号是对数据库的execution。挑战者生成针对求解器当前难度“hard but solvable”的SQL对，二者以GRPO交替更新，并用模板级重复惩罚防止多样性崩溃。在BIRD上无标签训练超过零样本baseline（3B提升6.6点，7B提升7.3点），甚至超越用人类gold标注训练的同配置模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练有竞争力的Text-to-SQL agent通常依赖人工标注的natural-language/SQL对，成本高昂、领域特定，是扩展到新数据库的瓶颈。作者希望证明在零标注对的情况下仍可训练出有竞争力的求解器，以打破标注瓶颈。

Method: SQL-Zero采用proposer-solver自我对弈框架：挑战者（challenger）与求解器（solver）从相同基座LLM出发，唯一ground truth是SQL在数据库上的执行结果。挑战者生成针对求解器当前水平的SQL对（校准到“困难但可解”的难度区间），使训练信号具有课程学习特性；两个角色用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）交替更新；同时对挑战者施加模板级重复惩罚以防止生成多样性崩塌。

Result: 在BIRD数据库上无标签训练，3B模型在BIRD dev上比零样本base提升6.6点，7B提升7.3点；且高于用人类BIRD gold在同一训练recipe下训练的匹配对照组。迁移性依赖规模：3B下每一轮迭代在未见过的Spider和Spider-Syn（词汇扰动）上仍优于base，且比gold对照组退化更少；而7B下仅第一轮迭代保留迁移能力。精确配对检验无法确证对gold对照组的优势。

Conclusion: 证明了在零人工标注条件下，通过基于执行的自我对弈可以训练出有竞争力的Text-to-SQL求解器。方法无需人工标注即可取得优于或媲美利用人类gold标注训练的模型效果，且在小规模模型上展现出更好的迁移性，为去标注化的Text-to-SQL训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Training a competitive Text-to-SQL agent usually depends on human-annotated natural-language/SQL pairs, which are expensive, domain-specific, and a bottleneck for scaling to new databases. We show it is possible to train a competitive solver with zero annotated pairs. We introduce SQL-Zero, a proposer-solver self-play in which a challenger and a solver start from the same base LLM and the only ground truth is execution against the database itself. The challenger generates SQL pairs calibrated to the solver's current difficulty (targeting "hard but solvable"), and both roles are updated with GRPO in alternating turns, with a template-level repetition penalty on the challenger to prevent diversity collapse. Training on BIRD databases with no labels, self-play improves over the zero-shot base on BIRD dev by 6.6 points at 3B and 7.3 points at 7B.
