### [166] [CUA-Universe: A Scalable and Dynamic Environment for Hybrid GUI+CLI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05374)
*Haoting Shi,Wenhao Wang,Weicheng Fang,Yaozhong Liang,Tian Jin,Pengxiang Zhao,Guangyi Liu,Siheng Chen,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CUA-Universe是一个规模化环境到数据的流水线，可将真实桌面软件转化为混合GUI+CLI环境，通过App-Forge、Task-Weave和Path-Steer三个模块分别实现环境适配、任务合成与轨迹优化，训练出的9B模型在多个基准上同时提升了任务成功率和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体主要依赖GUI操作，轨迹低效；而真实计算机工作本质上是混合的，需要同时协调视觉状态检查与命令行操作。但构建支持GUI和CLI双模式的混合环境需要大量人工工程；现有智能体也缺乏对两种界面的互补使用能力——CLI原生智能体缺乏视觉感知，GUI原生智能体在执行更适合命令行的操作时效率低下。

Method: 提出CUA-Universe数据流水线，包含三个核心组件：1) App-Forge将应用适配为可复现VM并通过发现、包装或生成命令行接口，扩展到16个应用；2) Task-Weave从可复用的种子文件操作中合成难度可控的多样化混合任务；3) Path-Steer引导智能体沿高效混合路径进行rollout并收集经过验证的轨迹用于后训练。

Result: 基于该数据训练的9B模型在多个基准上同时提升成功率与效率：CUA-Verse得分+39.3分，步骤减少37%，token减少60%；OSWorld成功率+16.8分，步骤减少57%，token减少44%；OSWorld-MCP得分+7.84分，步骤减少27%，token减少30%。

Conclusion: CUA-Universe验证了通过规模化构造混合GUI+CLI环境与数据，可以训练智能体从低效的纯GUI交互或脆弱的CLI脚本向有效的GUI+CLI协同编排转变，为更强大高效的计算机使用智能体提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Computer-use agents have advanced on benchmarks like OSWorld and AndroidWorld, but still act mostly through the GUI, often producing inefficient trajectories. Real-world computer work is hybrid, combining visual-state inspection with precise, high-throughput command-line operations, so capable agents must coordinate both modalities over shared application state. Yet scalable hybrid environments remain scarce because supporting both GUI and CLI over real applications typically requires substantial manual engineering for each application. Existing agents also struggle to use the two interfaces complementarily: CLI-native agents lack visual perception for tasks involving interface state or layout, while GUI-native agents are inefficient for operations better executed through commands. We introduce CUA-Universe, a scalable environment-to-data pipeline that turns real desktop software into hybrid GUI+CLI environments. App-Forge adapts applications into reproducible VMs and command-line surfaces it discovers, wraps, or generates, scaling to 16 applications; Task-Weave synthesizes diverse hybrid tasks of controllable difficulty from reusable operations over seed files; and Path-Steer steers rollouts along efficient hybrid paths and harvests verified trajectories for post-training. Training on this data shifts behavior from inefficient GUI interaction and brittle CLI scripting toward effective GUI+CLI orchestration. Our 9B model improves both success and efficiency on CUA-Verse (Score +39.3 pts; -37% steps, -60% tokens), OSWorld (SR +16.8 pts; -57% steps, -44% tokens), and OSWorld-MCP (Score +7.84 pts; -27% steps, -30% tokens). CUA-Universe provides a scalable path toward more capable and efficient computer-use agents.

</details>


### [167] [Molecular Déjà Vu: Digit-Level Retrieval of Published Values in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05381)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Christian J. Cyron,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对22个前沿大语言模型在12个分子回归基准上进行审计，发现逐字检索（verbatim retrieval）现象广泛存在但具有基准特异性。推理水平会显著改变检索行为（高推理水平下被标记频率比最低水平高89%），且抑制检索后不同模型的预测误差在相对意义上更接近，表明模型通用预测能力不完全取决于记忆值的多少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在分子性质基准上的评估日益普遍，但准确率无法区分模型是真正预测了分子性质还是从训练数据中检索出已发表的数值。这种混淆会严重高估LLM在分子属性预测任务上的真实能力，因此需要对逐字检索现象进行系统审计。

Method: 作者对22个前沿模型在12个分子回归基准上进行逐字检索审计，在不同推理水平下重复实验以考察推理对检索的影响，并通过变换SMILES字符串等方式打断检索路径进行对照实验。

Result: 逐字检索在5个数据集上超过50%的模型出现该现象，其余数据集上仅零星出现；高推理水平下检索被标记频率比最低水平高89%；即使使用变换后的SMILES字符串，最强模型仍能识别出原始标签的组合；抑制检索后各模型预测误差在相对意义上更加接近。

Conclusion: LLM在分子回归基准上的逐字检索是广泛但具有基准特异性的现象，推理水平影响检索行为，且模型间的预测差异部分源于各自不同的记忆化程度，而非纯预测能力差异。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on molecular property benchmarks, but accuracy cannot distinguish a model that predicts a property from one that retrieves a published number. We audit 22 frontier models on 12 regression benchmarks for verbatim retrieval and find that it is widespread but relatively benchmark-specific: on five datasets more than $50\%$ of the LLMs show verbatim retrieval, while on the remaining datasets it appears only in isolated cells. We run our experiments at two reasoning levels and find that reasoning changes retrieval. The same experiments, on the same molecules and with the same prompt, are flagged $89\%$ more often at the higher reasoning level than at the lowest one. Finally, we test a way to interrupt retrieval in our most contaminated cases, and find that the strongest models in some cases still recognise a combination of transformed SMILES strings and original labels. Furthermore, suppressing retrieval moves the prediction errors of the different models closer together in relative terms, while their differing use of verbatim retrieval spreads them apart. This indicates that the general predictive capability of an LLM is not determined solely by the amount of memorised values. This work provides an overview of the amount and depth of verbatim retrieval in molecular regression benchmarks using LLMs.

</details>


### [168] [Necessary or Sufficient? Evaluating LLM Explanations With Behavioural Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05385)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Nabeel Kemal,Ashwin Kandath,Owen O'Neill,Guillaume Bourgeon,Houssem Chatbri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了LLM在智能体工作流中输出的"命名因素"（named factors）解释是否与其可观察的决策行为一致。作者提出两种解释语义——必要性（改变某因素会改变输出）和充分性（保留某因素、移除其他可变信息后输出不变），并在两类合成任务（顾问推荐、提示词危害性判断）中对Claude、GPT、Gemini家族的8个模型进行黑盒干预测试。结果显示解释排名与必要性/充分性分数的平均Spearman相关系数仅为0.349~0.580，未被引用的因素在57.6%的顾问响应中得分高于最低的被引用因素。框架可作为个体LLM决策解释的黑盒可靠性检查，但被引用的前三个因素并不总能可靠地识别出实际影响最强的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体工作流中，操作者依赖LLM输出的解释（特别是命名的关键因素）来监控系统、诊断错误或决定是否升级输出。这种依赖隐含假设：解释与组件可观察的决策行为一致。然而该假设从未被严格检验。

Method: 作者形式化两种解释语义：必要性（改变某因素会改变输出）和充分性（保留该因素并移除其他可变信息时输出得以保持）。通过受控黑盒干预对每个被引用因素计算必要性分数（改变它时输出改变频率）和充分性分数（保留它时输出保持频率）。在两类合成任务（顾问推荐、提示词危害/风险判定）中，对Claude、GPT、Gemini家族的8个模型进行系统性评估。

Result: 被引用排名与必要性/充分性分数的平均Spearman相关性在顾问推荐任务中为0.349和0.354，在提示词监控任务中为0.431和0.580。未被引用的因素在57.6%（顾问·必要性）和58.1%（顾问·充分性）的响应中得分高于被引用因素中最低者；提示词监控对应的比率分别为25.8%和8.9%。被引用的前三个因素包含有用信息，但并不能可靠地识别出必要性或充分性语义下实际测量影响最强的三个因素。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种黑盒可靠性检查手段，用于评估智能体监督场景中解释的可信度，但研究范围限定于单个LLM决策而非整个工作流。核心结论是：LLM的解释虽包含一定信息量，但其与真实决策行为的对齐程度不足以保证操作者仅凭解释进行诊断或升级决策。

Abstract: LLM decision components that can operate within agent workflows often produce action-relevant recommendations or judgements together with explanations. Operators may use the named factors to monitor a system, diagnose errors, or decide when to escalate an output. Such use assumes that the explanations agree with the component's observable decision behaviour. We test two interpretations of the named factors: necessity, meaning that changing a factor would change the output, and sufficiency, meaning that retaining it while removing other changeable information would preserve the output. We evaluate these interpretations in two synthetic use cases: recommending advisors to clients and judging prompts for harmfulness or risk. Models return an output and the top three factors that most influenced it. Controlled black-box interventions estimate a necessity score for each factor by measuring how often changing it changes the output, and a sufficiency score by measuring how often retaining it preserves the output. Across eight models from the Claude, GPT, and Gemini families, the mean Spearman correlations between the cited ranking and the necessity and sufficiency scores are 0.349 and 0.354 for advisor recommendation, and 0.431 and 0.580 for prompt monitoring. Furthermore, an uncited factor scores above the lowest-scoring cited factor in 57.6% of advisor responses under necessity and 58.1% under sufficiency; the corresponding prompt-monitoring rates are 25.8% and 8.9%. The cited top three contain useful information but do not reliably identify the three factors with the strongest measured influence under necessity or sufficiency. The framework provides a black-box reliability check for explanations used in agent oversight while remaining scoped to individual LLM decisions.

</details>


### [169] [A Deep Generative Model for Synthesizing Labeled Wireless Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05396)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于深度学习的生成方法IIns-GAN（Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks），用于生成具有位置标签、可自适应不同环境场景的逼真无线信号，以解决真实无线传感数据采集与标注成本高昂的问题。在公开UWB数据集上的实验表明，生成信号既反映了真实测量的物理特性，又显著提升了多种无线感知任务（如距离估计、环境识别）的模型训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线感知领域高度依赖带位置标签的无线信号用于性能评估和模型训练，但真实数据集的采集和标注成本高昂。传统基于环境模型合成信号的方法需要大量超参数调优且仿真度不足，难以满足综合模型训练的需求，亟需一种更高效、更真实的合成带标签无线信号的方法。

Method: 提出IIns-GAN（Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks），一种基于深度学习的对抗生成网络方法，旨在生成具有真实物理特性且能自适应不同环境场景的带标签无线信号。该方法不同于传统环境建模合成方法，通过GAN架构学习真实无线信号的数据分布，并引入实例间（inter-instance）的信息以提升生成信号的多样性和适应性。

Result: 在公开Ultra-Wideband (UWB)数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明IIns-GAN生成的信号在物理特性上与真实世界测量数据高度一致，并且对多种无线感知任务（包括距离估计和环境识别）的模型训练有显著促进作用，验证了生成信号的真实性和实用性。

Conclusion: IIns-GAN作为一种新颖的DL-based无线信号合成方法，有效克服了传统合成方法的局限，在降低数据获取成本的同时生成高保真、环境自适应的标记无线信号，为无线感知领域的模型训练提供了可靠的数据增强解决方案。

Abstract: Wireless signals with position-related labels are pivotal for both performance evaluation and model training in the realm of wireless sensing. However, acquiring real-world datasets is often challenged by significant measurement and labeling costs. Traditional methods for synthesizing labeled wireless signals typically rely on environmental models, leading to extensive hyper-parameter tuning and inadequate realism for comprehensive model training purposes. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel deep learning (DL)-based method, namely Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks (IIns-GAN), to generate realistic labeled wireless signals. The generated signals are particularly adaptive to different environment scenarios and well-suited for various model training tasks, including distance estimation and environment identification. We have conducted extensive experiments on public Ultra-Wideband (UWB) datasets to evaluate the realism and utility of the generated signals. The results demonstrate that the signals generated by IIns-GAN mirror the physical characteristics of real-world measurements, and significantly contribute to the improvement of model training in diverse wireless sensing tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [At Equal Inference Cost, Multi-Agent Structure Does Not Beat a Single Frozen Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04217)
*David Dylan,Aoife Brennan,Cian Murphy,Niamh O'Sullivan,Conor Kelly,Saoirse Walsh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 在固定语言模型总调用次数的预算下，进化多智能体LLM团队（Planner-Executor-Critic）并未比进化单个智能体带来统计显著的收益提升。在ALFWorld上团队均值更高但p=0.80不显著，且其价值几乎完全来自executor角色；在WebShop上进化效果无效且团队表现更差。作者提出MA-Evolve方法，并得出结论：在等价推理成本下，多智能体结构增加成本而缺乏清晰收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明多智能体LLM流水线（如Planner-Executor-Critic）通常优于单智能体，但这种收益常伴随更高的推理成本——团队每步需多次模型调用。已有自动化方法在比较时给团队额外计算量（相同环境rollout数但每次调用更多），造成不公平比较。作者希望在同一总推理预算（相同语言模型调用次数）下，考察进化多智能体团队是否仍优于进化单智能体，以揭示多智能体结构是否真正带来价值。

Method: 提出MA-Evolve方法，将Planner-Executor-Critic团队表示为三个可进化的角色提示词（role prompts），通过每个角色的坐标上升（per-role coordinate ascent）在共享冻结的7B骨干模型上并行优化。同时进行对照组实验：在ALFWorld和WebShop上以固定总LM调用预算对比进化单executor与进化整个团队，并采用leave-one-in分析评估各角色的实际贡献。也进行了2-3倍额外计算量下团队表现的补充实验。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，进化单executor显著提升优于未进化智能体；完整团队平均分最高（0.769 vs 0.754），但p=0.80表明并不显著优于单智能体，且消耗了1.8倍的评估调用次数。Leave-one-in分析表明实际价值完全来自executor，planner和critic进化为空或低影响提示词，极少改变executor动作。在2-3倍免费计算下团队仅能与单智能体持平。WebShop上进化无效且团队趋势更差。

Conclusion: 在固定推理成本（相同LM调用次数）条件下，多智能体团队的结构性优势未能体现：团队相对单智能体的增益不具统计显著性，收益主要由executor单一角色贡献，而planner与critic几乎不产生实质作用。多智能体结构在等价推理预算下增加了推理成本但缺乏明确收益，提示研究者应在公平预算约束下重新审视多智能体架构的实际价值。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines, such as Planner-Executor-Critic teams, often report gains over single agents, but these gains usually come with higher inference cost because the team makes multiple model calls per environment step. Existing automated methods search over roles, topologies, and prompts, but typically compare teams against single agents at equal environment rollouts, giving the team extra compute. We instead fix the total number of language-model calls and ask whether evolving a multi-agent team still beats evolving a single agent under the same budget.
  We introduce MA-Evolve, which represents a Planner-Executor-Critic team as three evolvable role prompts and optimizes them by per-role coordinate ascent over a shared frozen 7B backbone. On ALFWorld, evolving a single executor significantly improves over the unevolved agent, while the full team achieves the highest mean but is not statistically better than the single agent: 0.769 versus 0.754, p = 0.80, despite using 1.8 times more evaluation calls. Leave-one-in analysis shows that the realized value comes entirely from the executor; the planner and critic evolve to empty or low-impact prompts and rarely change the executor's action. With 2-3 times free compute, the team only matches the single agent, and on WebShop evolution is null while the team trends worse. Under equal inference cost, multi-agent structure adds cost without clear benefit.

</details>


### [171] [Multi-dimensional Bias in Modeling Multi-dimensional Preferences: Evaluating the Ability of Synthetic Agents to Replace Human Participants in Conjoint Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04243)
*Ho Ting Hung,Nachiket Midha,Victor Y. Wu,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM合成代理能否在联合分析（conjoint）设计中复现人类多维偏好模式。通过复制已发表的联合实验，从表征对应、推断对应和程序稳定性三个维度对比合成代理与人类数据的结果，发现合成参与者的有效性是分层的、声称依赖的，不能简单替代人类样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在调查实验中被用于增强鲁棒性或降低数据采集成本的研究日益增多，但其在联合分析设计（政治学中日益流行的方法）中的有效性尚缺乏探讨。填补这一研究空白是本文的主要动因。

Method: 复制已发表的联合分析研究，让合成代理（由LLM驱动的虚拟受访者）完成相同的实验任务，并从三个维度与原始人类数据进行比较：(1) 表征对应——选择分布的对齐程度；(2) 推断对应——估计量的统计与实质性相似度；(3) 程序稳定性——结果的可重复性。

Result: 合成代理与人类数据在三个维度和不同复制研究中的表现参差不齐，结果不一致。在部分维度上虽有良好对齐，但整体效果不均匀。

Conclusion: 合成参与者的有效性应被视为声称依赖且分层的。能够复现图表或获得符号方向一致的结果仅是聚合输出相似的证据，不足以支持用合成代理替代人类受访者。学界需要在各层面系统绘制该创新的边界后，才能考虑将合成代理作为人类样本的稳健替代方案。

Abstract: Despite growing interest in using LLMs to add robustness or reduce data-collection costs in survey experiments, their efficacy in conjoint design---an increasingly popular method in political science---remains underexplored. This paper addresses that gap by investigating whether synthetic agents can reproduce the multi-dimensional human preference patterns that conjoint is designed to capture. It replicates published conjoint studies and compares the results generated by synthetic agents with original human data along three dimensions: representational correspondence, inferential correspondence, and procedural stability. Our analysis evaluates the alignment of choice distributions as well as the statistical and substantive similarity of estimates, and the results are uneven across these dimensions and studies replicated. This implies that the validity of synthetic participants should be considered claim-dependent and hierarchical. Reproducing a figure or obtaining strong sign agreement is evidence of similar aggregate outputs, but not enough to support replacing human respondents. Our results suggest that the discipline as a whole must first map this innovation's boundaries across various levels before considering synthetic agents a robust substitute for human samples.

</details>


### [172] [EVOHARNESSBENCH: Can Your Agents Keep Pace with an Evolving Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04280)
*Zixuan Ke,Vaidehi Patil,Haizhou Shi,Yang Li,Ye Liu,Sarath Shekkizhar,Anurag Koul,Jiayu Wang,Xuan Phi Nguyen,Semih Yavuz,Mohit Bansal,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出EVOHARNESSBENCH基准，用于评估智能体在受控的"工具/技能/智能体编排架构"(harness)演化下的表现。与现有把非平稳性放在任务流中的持续学习基准不同，该基准将非平稳性放在外部供给的harness本身，包含17个多阶段harness流、802个任务、520个工具、42个技能和62个智能体，揭示三种持续存在的性能缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于LLM的智能体依赖由工具、可复用技能和专用子智能体构成的"harness"来决定其观察与行为边界，且该harness会随新能力加入而持续演化。现有针对智能体的持续学习基准通常将非平稳性置于任务流中而保持harness固定，忽略了harness本身演化这一实际且重要的问题。

Method: 提出EVOHARNESSBENCH基准，通过基于验证器的基准确定性构建17个多阶段harness流，涵盖802个任务、520个工具、42个技能与62个智能体，并按三个轴（工具、技能、智能体）控制harness演化。评估两个互补设置：部署评估（隔离harness扩展时对既有可达能力的保留）与自演化自适应评估（测试新能力引入时累积经验是否仍有价值）。

Result: 揭示三个持续缺口：1) 仅扩展harness即可使先前已解决任务的性能下降，产生"harness诱导遗忘"；2) 自演化自适应的收益在harness演化阶段、能力轴和环境之间并不稳定；3) 保留与自适应可能方向相反——保留既有能力并不必然提升对新引入能力的自适应，反之亦然。

Conclusion: harness演化是与任务流演化本质不同的一项独特挑战，若要使智能体在随harness演化保持步调的同时保留先前有效的行为，需要针对性研究。该基准为此建立了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Modern LLM-based agents operate through a harness of tools, reusable skills, and specialist agents that shapes what they observe and what they can do. In practice, this harness continually evolves as new capabilities are added. We introduce EVOHARNESSBENCH, a benchmark for evaluating agents under controlled harness evolution across three axes (tools, skills, and agents). Unlike existing continual-learning benchmarks for agents, which typically place non-stationarity (i.e., what changes over time) in the task stream while keeping the harness fixed, EVOHARNESSBENCH places non-stationarity in the externally supplied harness itself. It contains 17 multi-stage harness streams constructed deterministically from verifier-based benchmarks, comprising 802 tasks, 520 tools, 42 skills, and 62 agents. We evaluate two complementary settings corresponding to the central challenges of harness evolution: deployment evaluation, which isolates retention of previously accessible competence as the harness expands, and self-evolving adaptation evaluation, which tests whether accumulated experience remains useful as new capabilities are introduced. Our results reveal three persistent gaps. First, harness expansion alone can degrade performance on previously solved tasks, producing harness-induced forgetting. Second, gains from self-evolving adaptation remain inconsistent across stages of harness evolution, capability axes, and environments. Third, retention and adaptation can pull in different directions: preserving earlier competence does not necessarily improve adaptation to newly introduced capabilities, and vice versa. These results establish harness evolution as a distinct challenge for building agents that can keep pace with an evolving harness while preserving previously effective behavior.

</details>


### [173] [Abstraction Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04303)
*Boning Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Abstraction Agent，一个零样本管道，利用大语言模型从自然语言游戏描述中发现连续战略特征，对私有状态进行评分并聚类为抽象桶，无需领域专用评估器、训练数据或博弈树遍历即可构建信息抽象，在HUNL翻牌后和ROVER Trials等游戏中将可利用性降低高达62%，并能零修改迁移到多种其他游戏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型不完美信息博弈中，信息抽象（将战略相似的私有状态分组为桶）是扩展博弈求解算法的关键。然而传统抽象构建依赖领域专用的评估器（如手牌强度计算器、权益估计器），这些工具需要专家知识和工程成本，且对研究较少的游戏不可用。作者旨在消除这种领域专用依赖，实现无需专家知识即可自动构建有效信息抽象的通用方案。

Method: 提出Abstraction Agent零样本管道，包含四个阶段：(1) 特征发现，使用LLM从自然语言游戏描述中结合校准锚点发现连续战略特征；(2) 批量私有状态评分，LLM对每个私有状态按发现的特征打分；(3) 基于相关性的特征选择，去除冗余特征；(4) k-means聚类将状态分入抽象桶。整个过程无需任何游戏专用评估器、训练数据，也无需在抽象构建期间进行博弈树遍历。

Result: 在HUNL翻牌后残局中，所生成的抽象相比期望手牌强度基线降低了高达62%的提升策略可利用性；在ROVER Trials（一个不在任何预训练语料库中的原创游戏）中，在每个粒度上均胜过标量排名基线。此外，该管道以不变的提示词成功迁移到四张牌底池限注奥马哈、HUNL翻前与翻牌、以及立直麻将，发现的特征与各游戏公认的战略概念一致。

Conclusion: 本文展示了将LLM参数中隐含的战略知识转换为显式数值特征用于下游算法计算的结构化知识抽取这一研究方向的有效性。Abstraction Agent作为零样本、无需领域专用评估器的信息抽象方法，为大型不完美信息博弈提供了通用且可迁移的抽象构建方案。

Abstract: Information abstraction, which groups strategically similar private states into a tractable number of buckets, is essential for scaling game-solving algorithms to large imperfect-information games. Constructing effective abstractions, however, has traditionally required domain-specific evaluators such as hand-strength calculators or equity estimators, which demand expert knowledge and engineering effort and are unavailable for most less-studied games. We propose the Abstraction Agent, a zero-shot pipeline that uses a large language model (LLM) to discover continuous strategic features from a natural-language game description, score private states on these features, and cluster them into abstraction buckets, without any game-specific evaluator, training data, or game-tree traversal during abstraction construction. The pipeline runs in four phases: feature discovery with calibration anchors, batched private-state scoring, correlation-based feature selection, and $k$-means clustering. The resulting abstractions reduce lifted-strategy exploitability by up to 62% relative to an expected-hand-strength baseline on heads-up no-limit Texas hold'em (HUNL) turn endgames, and beat a scalar rank baseline at every granularity on ROVER Trials, an original game absent from any pretraining corpus. Beyond these quantitative benchmarks, the pipeline transfers with unchanged prompts to four-card Pot-Limit Omaha, HUNL preflop and flop, and Riichi Mahjong, where the discovered features track each game's recognized strategic concepts. This is structured knowledge elicitation: converting implicit strategic knowledge in LLM parameters into explicit numerical features for downstream algorithmic computation. The code is available at https://github.com/lbn187/AbstractionAgent.

</details>


### [174] [Online Change-point Detection for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05298)
*Fatemeh Saberi Khomami,Julita Vassileva*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出PPR（Patterns of Past Rewards），一种轻量级、算法无关的在线变点检测方法，用于合作式多智能体强化学习（MARL）训练中检测环境或任务目标的非平稳变化。PPR通过对回报流进行平滑、突出近期变化并应用统计漂移检测器来标记显著变化，在自定义的Speaker-Listener环境中验证了其能够兼顾检测速度与报警稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作式MARL系统依赖过往经验学习协调行为，但当环境或任务目标在训练过程中发生变化时，这些经验可能变得不可靠。智能体需要首先识别情境变化，再决定如何适应，因此需要一种能够在线检测变化的机制。

Method: 提出PPR（Patterns of Past Rewards）：一种轻量级、算法无关的检测器。它平滑智能体的回报流，突出近期变化，并应用统计漂移检测器来标记显著变化。在基于Multi-Agent Particle Environment构建的Speaker-Listener环境中，设计了两种受控非平稳场景进行评估。

Result: 实验显示检测速度与报警稳定性之间存在权衡：平滑回报基线能更早检测但产生大量重复报警；而直接对原始回报应用检测器常常漏检变化。PPR能限制冗余检测，同时可靠识别受控变化，实现更平衡的性能。

Conclusion: PPR作为一种轻量级的基于回报的监控工具，能够使合作式MARL系统在训练过程中可靠地识别重大变化，从而为后续的自适应策略调整奠定基础。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems rely on past experience for learning coordinated behaviour, but this experience may become unreliable if the environment or task objective changes during training. In such cases, agents first need a way to recognize that the situation has changed before deciding how to adapt. This paper studies online change-point detection for cooperative MARL using reward-derived signals. We propose \emph{Patterns of Past Rewards} (PPR), a lightweight algorithm-agnostic detector that smooths agents' return streams, highlights recent changes, and applies a statistical drift detector to flag significant shifts. We evaluate PPR in a custom Speaker-Listener environment based on the Multi-Agent Particle Environment under two controlled non-stationarity scenarios. Our results show a trade-off between detection speed and alarm stability. A smoothed-return baseline detects earlier but produces many repeated alarms. In contrast, applying the detector directly to raw returns often misses the shift. PPR offers a more balanced approach by limiting redundant detections while still identifying the controlled shifts. These findings highlight PPR as a lightweight, reward-based monitoring tool that enables cooperative MARL systems to reliably identify major changes during training.

</details>


### [175] [Trust-Aware Adaptive Disclosure for Inference Privacy Preservation in Multi-Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05340)
*Puspanjali Ghoshal,Tobias J. Oechtering*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统中的隐私保护一致性（consensus）问题，提出了一种基于信任感知的隐私控制框架（Trust-Aware Privacy Control），通过信任依赖的随机策略自适应地控制消息披露，在保持共识性能的同时降低对手对智能体潜在目标的推断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统越来越多地部署在信息关键系统中（如医疗管理、智能电网），每个智能体都有需要对外部观察者隐藏的潜在目标。然而现有方法缺乏对智能体间动态信任关系的考量，如何在目标推断攻击下实现隐私保护与共识性能的平衡是待解决的问题。

Method: 提出Trust-Aware Privacy Control框架，基于智能体间动态信任关系自适应地调整消息披露策略。具体地，通过一种信任依赖的随机策略来控制信息释放，从而在共识性能与隐私保护之间实现权衡。

Result: 实验表明，与代表性基线方法相比，该方法在保持有竞争力的共识效用的同时，显著降低了对手的目标推断准确率，验证了信任感知机制在多智能体系统隐私保护中的有效性。

Conclusion: 信任感知的隐私控制机制能够在目标推断攻击场景下有效实现隐私保护，同时维持良好的共识性能，为多智能体系统中的隐私与性能权衡提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Agent based systems are increasingly deployed in information critical systems including healthcare management systems, and smart grids. In this paper, we consider a multi-agent system where each agent has a latent goal that needs to be kept hidden from observing adversaries. More specifically, this paper studies privacy-preserving consensus in networked multi-agent systems under goal inference attacks. We propose a Trust-Aware Privacy Control framework that adapts message disclosure based on the dynamic trust relationships between agents. The proposed method controls information release using a trust-dependent stochastic policy. This enables a tradeoff between consensus performance and privacy preservation. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method reduces adversarial goal inference accuracy compared to representative baselines, while maintaining competitive consensus utility, thereby highlighting the effectiveness of trust-aware mechanisms in privacy preservation of the agents in multi-agent systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [SAGE: Semantic Attribute Graphs for Multi-Entity Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04255)
*Yongjoo Kim,Mincheol Kwon,Seonga Choi,Minseung Lee,Kyeong-Jin Oh,Hyunyoung Lee,Yunsu Choi,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE，一种无需训练的框架，通过从稠密文档图像中解析语义实体、以多向量嵌入的层级图节点表示并通过迭代子图匹配进行检索，解决标准视觉语言检索器在密集文档细粒度检索中的"语义稀释"问题；同时引入DEAR基准数据集（1,055个query-image对，来自产品详情页），实验表明SAGE在实体级细粒度视觉比较查询上显著优于patch级和OCR基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密文档图像包含大量细粒度视觉和文本实体，其相关性取决于用户查询。标准视觉语言检索器用单向量编码裁剪区域，会混淆不同实体信号并掩盖细粒度检索所需证据。作者将这种失效模式称为"语义稀释"，并定量证明它会随实体密度增加而降低实体级检索性能。

Method: SAGE是一个无需训练的框架，包含三部分：(1) 从稠密文档图像中解析语义实体；(2) 将实体表示为带有多向量嵌入的层级图节点；(3) 通过迭代的实体级子图匹配来检索与查询相关的证据。同时构造了DEAR数据集，包含1,055个query-image对，来自产品详情页，涵盖四种递进复杂度的查询类型，要求从视觉密集的输入中检索并比较多个细粒度实体。

Result: 实验表明SAGE显著降低语义稀释的影响，在DEAR上优于patch级和OCR基线检索方法。在多实体视觉比较查询上取得了Recall@3为0.849和生成得分2.746的成绩。

Conclusion: SAGE通过层级图结构和多向量嵌入有效解决了稠密文档图像中细粒度实体检索的语义稀释问题，在无需训练的前提下超越了现有基线，验证了结构化语义解析在多实体视觉查询任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Dense document images often contain many fine-grained visual and textual entities whose relevance depends on a user query. Standard vision-language retrievers encode cropped regions with a single vector, which can mix distinct entity signals and obscure the evidence needed for fine-grained retrieval. We call this failure mode Semantic Dilution and quantitatively show that it degrades entity-level retrieval as a function of entity density. To mitigate it, we propose SAGE, a training-free framework that parses semantic entities from dense document images, represents them as hierarchical graph nodes with multi-vector embeddings, and retrieves query-relevant evidence through iterative entity-level subgraph matching. We also introduce DEAR, a dataset of 1,055 query--image pairs sourced from product detail pages, where each query requires retrieving and comparing multiple fine-grained entities from visually dense inputs across four question types of increasing complexity. Experiments show that SAGE substantially reduces semantic dilution and outperforms patch-level and OCR-based retrieval baselines on DEAR, achieving a Recall@3 of 0.849 and a generation score of 2.746 on multi-entity visual comparison queries. Our code is available at https://github.com/All4Nothing/SAGE.

</details>


### [177] [MURAL: Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04574)
*Ahmad Mousavi,Majid Alikhani,Yeon-Chang Lee,Roberto Corizzo,Yeganeh Abdollahinejad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MURAL提出一个统一的多模态图神经网络推荐框架，通过自适应边学习（Adaptive Edge Learner）和不确定性感知融合（Uncertainty-Aware Fusion）分别解决结构刚性与语义脆弱性问题，将多模态推荐从固定的结构增强转向动态拓扑发现，在TikTok和Amazon等大规模基准上超越多种SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统的多模态图神经网络主要依赖静态预计算的相似度图，无法适应不断演化的用户偏好（结构性刚性问题）；同时多模态特征信号充满噪声，融合过程不分青红皂白会扭曲协同信号（语义脆弱性问题）。这两大瓶颈严重制约了多模态推荐的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: MURAL包含三个核心模块：(1) 自适应边学习器（Adaptive Edge Learner），结合可微检索增强策略与近似近邻搜索（ANN）动态发现商品-商品间的潜在关联，复杂度为O(NlogN)；(2) 不确定性感知融合模块（Uncertainty-Aware Fusion），建模异构模态的任意不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty），动态降低不可靠特征的权重并优先高置信信号以抵御跨模态噪声；(3) 对比式师生对齐机制（contrastive teacher-student alignment），将模态专属表示锚定到稳定的行为信号上，在避免梯度泄漏的同时确保优化稳定性。

Result: 在TikTok、Amazon等大规模基准上的实验表明，MURAL在准确率上显著超越结构型和生成型的SOTA基线方法；同时通过领域特定的模态主导性（domain-specific modality dominance）提供了可解释性，并且在极端数据损坏条件下展现出较强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MURAL验证了将多模态推荐从静态结构增强转向动态拓扑发现的有效性。通过自适应边发现与不确定性感知融合的双重机制，该框架不仅提升了推荐性能，还兼顾了计算可扩展性、可解释性与鲁棒性，为多模态推荐系统的设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Graph Neural Networks have become standard for recommendation by augmenting sparse interaction data with content features. Yet current architectures face two bottlenecks: structural rigidity, from a reliance on static precomputed similarity graphs that cannot adapt to evolving preferences; and semantic fragility, where noisy modality signals are indiscriminately fused, distorting the collaborative signal. We propose MURAL (Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning), a unified framework that shifts multimodal recommendation from fixed structural augmentation to dynamic topology discovery. To address structural rigidity, an Adaptive Edge Learner combines a differentiable retrieval-augmented strategy with an approximate nearest neighbor search to discover latent item-item correlations that are both semantically adaptive and computationally scalable (O(NlogN)). To address semantic fragility, an Uncertainty-Aware Fusion module models the aleatoric uncertainty of heterogeneous modalities, dynamically down-weighting unreliable features while prioritizing high-confidence signals as a defense against cross-modal noise. We further employ a contrastive teacher-student alignment that anchors modality-specific representations to stable behavioral signals, ensuring optimization stability without gradient leakage. Experiments on large-scale benchmarks including TikTok and Amazon show that MURAL significantly surpasses both structural and generative state-of-the-art baselines, achieving superior accuracy while offering interpretability through domain-specific modality dominance and robustness under extreme data corruption.

</details>


### [178] [Latent-Aligned Reasoning for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04645)
*Jiarui Jin,Anyang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LARK（Latent-Aligned Reasoning frameworK），一个两阶段的潜在推理框架，用于解决多模态大语言模型（VLM）应用于推荐系统时的"跨模态稀释"（cross-modal dilution）问题——即在多步推理过程中视觉和文本信号逐步衰减的现象。LARK通过在第一阶段将可学习潜在token与链式思维（CoT）推理交错，并与冻结的视觉编码器显式对齐作为"视觉检查点"保留感知细节；第二阶段通过桥接MLP投影并利用item-to-item对比学习训练，同时将中间特征与第一阶段CoT隐藏状态对齐以锚定推理语义。在三个公开基准和一个工业数据集上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在多模态理解上表现优异，但应用于推荐时存在一个根本性挑战：当表征经过多步推理传播时，视觉和文本信号会逐步衰减，作者将其命名为"跨模态稀释"（cross-modal dilution），这导致模型在推荐场景中难以保留关键的多模态细节用于准确的item表征学习。

Method: LARK是一个两阶段潜在推理框架，集成在单一VLM中。第一阶段：可学习潜在token与多步CoT推理交错排列，并通过与冻结视觉编码器的显式对齐作为"视觉检查点"，在推理链中保留感知细节。第二阶段：通过桥接MLP对潜在表征进行投影，采用item-to-item对比学习进行训练；为防止推理语义消失，将中间特征与第一阶段的CoT隐藏状态对齐，使最终嵌入锚定在模型自身的推理输出上。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集和一个工业数据集上的实验表明，LARK在多种推荐架构下均取得了最先进的性能；受控消融实验证实了每个组件的独立贡献。

Conclusion: LARK通过采用潜在对齐推理机制有效缓解了VLM在推荐任务中的跨模态稀释问题，利用两阶段互补的对齐策略（视觉检查点+推理语义锚定）显著提升了多模态推荐性能，并在多组数据集与架构上验证了方法的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in cross-modal understanding, yet a fundamental challenge persists when applying them to recommendation: as representations propagate through multi-step reasoning, both visual and textual signals progressively attenuate - a phenomenon we term cross-modal dilution. To address this, we propose LARK (Latent-Aligned Reasoning frameworK), a two-stage latent reasoning framework with complementary alignment mechanisms within a single VLM. In the first stage, learnable latent tokens are interleaved with multi-step chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and explicitly aligned with a frozen vision encoder, serving as visual checkpoints that preserve perceptual details throughout the reasoning chain. In the second stage, the latent representations are projected via a bridge MLP and trained with item-to-item contrastive learning; to prevent the reasoning semantics from fading, intermediate features are aligned with the CoT hidden states from the first stage, anchoring the final embeddings to the model's own reasoning output. Experiments on three public benchmarks and one industrial dataset show that LARK achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple recommendation architectures, with controlled ablations confirming the distinct contribution of each component.

</details>


### [179] [VizIt: A multi-view framework for exploring single-cell, spatial, and genetic data online](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04658)
*Chenhang Christopher Zhang,Yanqing Lou,Jie Yuan,Mingming Lu,Jacob Parker,Himanshu Chintalapudi,Zechuan Lin,Clemens R. Scherzer,Yuxuan Hu,Ruifeng Hu,Xianjun Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VizIt是一个开源框架，用于单细胞和空间转录组学、表观基因组学和遗传学数据的多视图交互式探索，通过连接基因、细胞类型、条件、空间、基因组区域和变异中心视角，实现跨生物学视角的无缝导航，并以帕金森细胞图谱作为演示案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学研究日益需要从互补的生物学视角综合审视数据，但现有的交互式探索工具在不同模态和工具之间仍然碎片化，缺乏统一的多视角探索能力。

Method: VizIt框架将基因、细胞类型、条件、空间、基因组区域和变异中心等多种视角相互连接，支持多组学数据的无缝导航与交互式探索，并构建了可定制的帕金森细胞图谱作为多组学交互资源。

Result: 通过帕金森细胞图谱这一多组学交互式资源展示了VizIt的能力，该资源支持跨模态、跨视角的数据导航与定制化探索。

Conclusion: VizIt填补了多组学数据跨视角交互式探索的空白，为研究者提供了一个统一且开放的平台，可将不同生物学视角整合到同一分析工作流中。

Abstract: Multi-omic studies increasingly require data to be examined from complementary biological perspectives, yet interactive exploration remains fragmented across modalities and tools. We present VizIt, an open-source framework for multi-view exploration of single-cell and spatial transcriptomic, epigenomic and genetic data. VizIt connects gene-, cell type-, condition-, spatial-, genomic region- and variant-centered views, enabling seamless navigation across biological perspectives. We demonstrate VizIt through the Parkinson's Cell Atlas, a customizable interactive multi-omic resource.

</details>


### [180] [Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization for Training-Free AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04813)
*Wei Zhou,Tiandeng Wu,Jiandong Ding,Zhufeng Fan,Yi Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出IGPO（Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization），一种面向固定AI搜索流水线的免训练优化方法。IGPO将策略与库存事实分离，通过在线探测库存构建"库存画像"并注入策略指南，离线通过随机回滚按查询分组获取对比信号及库存引导的探索循环。自2026年5月起在商用智能助手的AI搜索系统中部署，14天在线A/B测试显示相对CTR提升3.17%，经审计的bad case减少38.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI搜索系统在部署初期面对频繁更新的产品目录，商品及其属性并非稳定知识，无法编码进固定提示词或策略中。微调、强化学习和静态提示修补均不适用：标签稀缺、奖励随库存漂移、模型发布成本高、提示修复易过时。需要一个无需训练、能适应动态库存环境的策略优化方案。

Method: IGPO采用以下核心设计：(1) 策略与库存事实分离——学习"策略指南"（Policy Guidelines）用于指导基于运行时库存证据的行动，而非记忆可用商品；(2) 在线阶段，每查询通过探测库存构建"库存画像"，并将相关策略指南注入检索与选择的提示词中；(3) 离线阶段，随机回滚按查询分组——混合结果组直接产生对比信号；(4) 库存引导的探索循环能区分"遗漏的检索路径"与"在该库存证据下确实无匹配支撑"两种情况。

Result: IGPO自2026年5月起已在商用智能助手的AI搜索系统中部署。为期14天的完整IGPO方案在线A/B测试显示：相对CTR提升3.17%，经审计的bad case减少38.9%。

Conclusion: IGPO证明了在不进行模型训练或固定提示修补的前提下，通过将策略与库存事实分离并利用库存证据进行策略级优化，可以显著改善动态商品目录环境下AI搜索系统的效果，同时大幅降低bad case。

Abstract: Early in deployment, an AI search system typically operates over a frequently updated product catalog, so the available items and their properties cannot be treated as stable knowledge that can be encoded in fixed prompts or strategies. Fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and static prompt patches fit poorly: labels are scarce, rewards drift with inventory, model releases are costly, and prompt fixes quickly stale. We present Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization (IGPO), a training-free approach for fixed AI search pipelines. IGPO separates policy from environment facts: it learns Policy Guidelines for acting on runtime inventory evidence rather than memorizing available items. Online, IGPO grounds each query by probing the inventory and constructing an inventory portrait, then injects relevant Policy Guidelines into the retrieval and selection prompts. Offline, stochastic rollouts are grouped by query -- mixed outcome groups directly yield contrastive signal, and an inventory-guided exploration loop distinguishes missed retrieval routes from cases where no matching support is found under the observed inventory evidence. Since May 2026, IGPO has been deployed in a commercial smart-assistant AI search system. A 14-day online A/B test of the complete IGPO treatment shows a 3.17% relative CTR lift and a 38.9% reduction in audited bad cases.

</details>


### [181] [Personalized Task Dependency Graphs for Mitigating Signal Erosion in Multi-Task Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04862)
*Fuyuan Liu,Tiandeng Wu,Yaqun Fang,Wei Zhou,Zehao Zhou,Wenping Chen,Qishun Mei,Jiaxin Zhou,Heng Chang,Yi Cao,Jiandong Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化任务依赖图（PTDG）框架，通过低秩近似动态调整物品级任务依赖强度并采用GCN消息传播与硬因果掩码建立自适应信息通路，同时引入自适应渐进掩码（APM）按任务稀疏度解耦共享参数，从而缓解工业推荐多目标优化中固定转换漏斗导致的信号衰减问题。在KuaiRand1K和工业数据集上，稀疏转化任务AUC提升最高1.45%，在线A/B测试中CVR提升1.2%、eCPM提升1.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐中的多目标优化面临两大核心挑战：一是刚性架构导致信号侵蚀，二是现有MTL方法在静态转换漏斗上施加统一的依赖强度，忽视了任务间相关性随物品特征自然变化的事实。沿固定链路的层级消息传递会造成累积信号衰减，严重损害稀疏深漏斗目标的性能。

Method: 提出PTDG框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 在遵守物理因果约束（如点击→支付）的前提下，通过低秩逼近为每个物品动态重连任务依赖路径的强度以保证结构鲁棒性；(2) 基于GCN的消息传播配合硬因果掩码，建立自适应的信息捷径；(3) 引入自适应渐进掩码（APM）策略，根据任务稀疏度对共享参数进行解耦，以稳定优化过程。

Result: 在KuaiRand1K和工业数据集上，PTDG在稀疏转化任务上的AUC最高提升1.45%，同时稠密目标性能保持相当；在线A/B测试中，相比基线CVR提升1.2%，eCPM提升1.9%。

Conclusion: PTDG通过动态个性化的任务依赖图重连与自适应参数解耦，有效克服了固定转换漏斗下层级消息传递的信号衰减问题，在保持稠密目标任务性能的同时显著改善了稀疏深层转化目标的预测精度，为工业推荐中的多目标优化提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Optimizing multiple conversion objectives is a core challenge in industrial recommendation, often limited by signal erosion in rigid architectures. Existing Multi-Task Learning (MTL) methods typically enforce uniform dependency strengths across a static conversion funnel, overlooking how task correlations naturally vary based on item characteristics. Hierarchical message passing along these fixed chains leads to cumulative signal attenuation, which degrades performance on sparse, deep-funnel objectives. To address this, we propose the Personalized Task Dependency Graphs (PTDG). While respecting necessary physical causal constraints (e.g., Click -> Pay), PTDG dynamically "rewires" the intensity of dependency pathways for each item via low-rank approximation to ensure structural robustness. We implement a GCN-based propagation with hard causal masking to establish adaptive information shortcuts. Additionally, we introduce an Adaptive Progressive Masking (APM) strategy that decouples shared parameters according to task sparsity, helping to stabilize optimization. Experiments on KuaiRand1K and an industrial dataset show that PTDG significantly improves AUC on sparse conversion tasks by up to 1.45%, while maintaining comparable performance on dense objectives. Online A/B testing shows PTDG improves Conversion Rate (CVR) by 1.2% and effective Cost Per Mille (eCPM) by 1.9% relative to the baseline.

</details>


### [182] [AtomRec: Evolving Atomic Memory for Agentic Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04882)
*Peiyu Hu,Weihai Lu,Siying Gu,Zhuodong Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Yuean Niu,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AtomRec，一种具有演化原子协作记忆的智能体推荐系统。它将用户和物品记忆组织为结构化原子单元并构建语义链接，新交互到来时演化相关历史字段，推荐时通过多跳证据路径检索链接记忆以支持可解释排序。在四个公开基准上平均相对提升约8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的智能体推荐系统的记忆机制通常将用户和物品信息压缩为粗略摘要，并以标量协作链接连接，难以保留细粒度的偏好阶段或在用户兴趣演化时检索可解释证据。

Method: AtomRec将用户和物品记忆表示为结构化原子单元，在不同记忆之间构建语义链接，并在新交互到达时演化相关历史字段。推荐阶段检索链式记忆作为多跳证据路径（而非孤立的邻居摘要），使协作信号支持基于依据的排序。

Result: 在四个公开基准上，AtomRec一致优于最先进的智能体与记忆增强基线，各指标平均相对提升约8.5%。

Conclusion: AtomRec通过演化式原子协作记忆与多跳证据路径检索，同时提升了推荐准确性与记忆机制的可解释性。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems use large language models to maintain semantic memory and support evidence-aware recommendation. However, existing memory mechanisms often compress user and item information into coarse summaries and connect them with scalar collaborative links, making it difficult to preserve fine-grained preference stages or retrieve interpretable evidence as user interests evolve. We propose \textsc{AtomRec}, an agentic recommender with evolving atomic collaborative memory. \textsc{AtomRec} represents user and item memories as structured atomic units, builds semantic links across related memories, and evolves related historical fields when new interactions arrive. During recommendation, it retrieves linked memories as multi-hop evidence paths rather than isolated neighbor summaries, allowing collaborative signals to support grounded ranking. Experiments on four public benchmarks show that \textsc{AtomRec} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art agentic and memory-augmented baselines, with around 8.5\% average relative improvement across metrics.

</details>


### [183] [SAM-D2Q: Aligning Multimodal Doc2Query with Search Demand and Conversion for E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04961)
*Hui Zhou,Jian Hui Ji,Lei Ma,Rong Xiao,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SAM-D2Q框架，一种面向电商搜索的、业务对齐的多模态Doc2Query文档扩展方法，将产品标题、图像和用户查询通过多模态理解与强化学习偏好对齐相结合，在AliExpress生产系统中实现GMV +3.38%和Pay Count +2.27%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索中用户查询与商家产品标题之间存在词汇不匹配问题，因为短标题无法覆盖多样化的用户表达和视觉产品属性。传统Doc2Query方法仅基于文本且未针对电商商业目标（如转化率、GMV）进行优化，可能生成语义合理但商业无效的扩展词，且遗漏产品图像中的关键属性。

Method: SAM-D2Q包含三个阶段：(1) 任务适配的多模态监督微调，增强对产品标题、图像和用户查询的视觉-语言理解；(2) 多模态数据增强，提升对关键视觉属性的感知及扩展覆盖；(3) 基于强化学习的偏好对齐，面向电商搜索商业目标，引导模型生成更匹配用户意图和商业价值的伪查询。整个框架运行在布尔检索约束下。

Result: 离线实验表明SAM-D2Q相比传统Doc2Query方法在检索性能上有显著提升；在AliExpress生产搜索系统中部署后，在线业务指标提升：GMV增加+3.38%，Pay Count增加+2.27%。

Conclusion: 将多模态信息与业务目标对齐相结合进行文档扩展，可有效解决电商搜索中的词汇不匹配问题，并在真实生产环境中带来显著的商业收益，验证了面向商业指标优化文档扩展模型的有效性。

Abstract: E-commerce search often suffers from vocabulary mismatch between user queries and merchant-authored product titles, since short titles cannot fully cover diverse user expressions or visual product attributes. Although Doc2Query alleviates this issue by generating pseudo-queries for document expansion, traditional methods are text-only and not optimized for e-commerce business objectives. As a result, they may produce semantically plausible but commercially ineffective expansions and miss key attributes present in product images. To this end, we propose E-commerce Search-Aligned Multimodal Doc2Query (SAM-D2Q), a business-aligned multimodal document expansion framework for e-commerce search under Boolean retrieval constraints. SAM-D2Q consists of three stages: (1) task-adapted multimodal supervised fine-tuning to enhance vision-language understanding of product titles, images, and user queries; (2) multimodal data augmentation to improve perception of key visual attributes and expansion coverage; and (3) reinforcement-learning-based preference alignment toward search business objectives, encouraging the model to generate pseudo-queries that better match user intent and commercial value. Offline experiments show that SAM-D2Q substantially improves retrieval performance over traditional Doc2Query methods. Deployed in the AliExpress production search system, SAM-D2Q improves online business metrics, increasing GMV by +3.38% and Pay Count by +2.27%.

</details>


### [184] [Repeated Queries Exhaust an LLM's Brand Recommendations but Not Its Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05059)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究重复相同购买问题是否会耗尽语言模型的品牌推荐。通过300个问答-引擎组合（50个问题、6个引擎、每项15次运行、对1470个评判组织的开放抽取），发现无检索的5个引擎在第15次运行时仍有86-92%的组合在产生从未见过的品牌（品牌库中位数15-31个）；唯一启用检索的引擎在第10次运行后出现饱和（品牌库中位数8个，64%的组合仍新增）。引文域名累积在所有测试范围内持续上升。单次运行仅覆盖五轮品牌集合的62-77%，各引擎中位抽取38个组织，其中15个仅出现在单一引擎。研究使用精确稀化法和Chao2丰富度估计，并通过固定名册抽取对照证明：名册受限追踪会人为制造平台期，开放抽取则消除了这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在重复相同的购买问询场景下，语言模型的品牌推荐是否会随时间收敛耗尽，并检验"固定名册"（fixed-roster）抽取方法相比"开放抽取"（open extraction）是否会在评估中人为制造虚假的平台期或饱和假象。

Method: 构建300个"问题-引擎"单元（50个问题×6个引擎×每单元15次运行），对1470个经裁决的组织进行开放抽取式信息提取；设置4个深度单元（web搜索引擎）运行24次以检验长尾饱和。采用精确稀化法（exact rarefaction）和Chao2丰富度估计器量化品牌发现的累积曲线，并设立固定名册抽取作为平行对照组。

Result: 无检索引擎在15次运行后仍高度活跃（86-92%的单元继续新增新品牌），品牌库中位数高达15-31个组织；而启用检索的引擎在第10轮即趋饱和（品牌库中位数8个，但仍有64%单元持续新增）。深度单元在24次运行时仍在新增引文域名，仅观测到Chao2下界估计的59-84%。单次运行仅能捕获五轮品牌集合的62-77%。各引擎中位抽取38个组织，其中中位15个为单一引擎独有。固定名册对照在相同响应上呈现完全平坦的曲线，证实名册边界造成了平台期假象。

Conclusion: 重复提问下，不依赖web检索的语言模型品牌推荐并未真正耗尽，所见饱和是固定名册评估方法的人为产物；开放抽取揭示品牌和引文域名在重复运行中持续涌现，说明需要引入丰富度导向的评估指标来真实度量语言模型的推荐多样性。

Abstract: Whether repeated identical buying questions exhaust a language model's brand recommendations depends on retrieval. Across 300 question-engine cells (50 questions, six engines, 15 runs each, open extraction over 1,470 adjudicated organizations), the five engines answering without web search were still adding never-seen brands at run 15 in 86-92% of cells, with median repertoires of 15-31 organizations; the one retrieval-enabled engine closed its list (median 8 organizations, 64% of cells still adding), matching four earlier deep cells where web-search runs saturated by run ten. Cited-domain accumulation keeps rising at every horizon tested: four deep cells were still adding domains at run 24 with 59-84% of the Chao2 lower-bound estimate observed, and 44% of the retrieval engine's breadth cells were still adding domains at run 15. A single run shows 62-77% of the five-run brand set, and across engines the median question draws 38 organizations, of which a median of 15 appear in exactly one engine. Estimators are exact rarefaction and Chao2 richness; a parallel fixed-roster extraction reproduces flat curves on identical responses, so roster-bounded tracking manufactures plateaus that open extraction removes.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Co-purchase Relation: Evolution of Complementary Recommendations at Allegro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05063)
*Aleksandra Osowska-Kurczab,Klaudia Nazarko,Eliška Kosturová,Lidia Wojciechowska,Michał Bień*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AlleCompanion，一个部署在Allegro.com的生产级互补产品推荐检索框架。该框架将嘈杂的行为信号转化为精确的语义互补关系，通过数据级过滤启发式结合类别约束的双塔架构（Category Adapter）有效过滤共购噪声；同时引入ComCat互补类别映射层，融合专家规则、人类反馈、LLM推理和统计挖掘来约束推荐边界。实际服务超过2000万月活用户，在有机发现和付费推广位均带来显著GMV提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的推荐模型通常无法区分"仅仅被一起购买"的商品与"真正能配合使用"的商品，例如相机和镜头是互补的，而同类型的另一台相机可能只是被相似用户一起购买。这种共购行为中的内在噪声严重干扰了互补产品推荐的准确性与用户实际需求的匹配度。

Method: 提出AlleCompanion框架：1) 数据层面，采用过滤启发式方法缓解大规模共购流量中的噪声；2) 模型层面，构建类别约束的双塔架构（Two Tower），其中Category Adapter在嵌入空间内引导模型将候选限定在逻辑上互补的类别边界内；3) 引入ComCat（Complementary Categories Mapping）多源互补类别映射层，作为翻译层从嘈杂流量中提炼有意义模式，整合专家规则、人机反馈（human-in-the-loop）、基于LLM的推理和统计挖掘，提供可维护且可控的解决方案。

Result: 实验结果表明，将显式类别级约束与神经架构相结合，能有效过滤共购噪声，从而推荐出满足真实用户需求的产品。该框架在Allegro.com服务超过2000万月活用户，在有机发现渠道中归因GMV显著提升，并在付费推广位带来可观的收入增长。

Conclusion: 将显式的类别级语义约束与神经推荐架构相结合，可以有效缓解大规模行为数据中的共购噪声问题。同时，通过引入融合多源知识（专家规则、LLM、人类反馈、统计方法）的可控映射层，能够在保持推荐质量的前提下实现生产环境的可维护性与业务可控性，验证了该方案在工业级互补推荐场景中的有效性。

Abstract: When a customer adds a professional camera to their cart, should the system suggest a matching lens, a generic tripod, or another camera body? Complementary Product Recommendation is vital for comprehensive basket building, yet standard models often fail to distinguish between items that are merely bought together and those that truly work together. In this paper, we present AlleCompanion: a production-scale retrieval framework deployed at Allegro.com that transforms noisy behavioural signals into precise semantic compatibility. We mitigate the intrinsic noise in large-scale co-purchase traffic by combining data-level filtering heuristics with a category-constrained Two Tower architecture. Within this framework, the Category Adapter guides the model in the embedding space, constraining candidates within logically complementary boundaries. Since modelling authentic user behaviour at scale is inherently difficult, we introduce ComCat, a multi-source Complementary Categories Mapping. ComCat acts as a translational layer that distils meaningful patterns from noisy traffic into a maintainable and controllable solution, integrating expert rules, human-in-the-loop feedback, LLM-based reasoning, and statistical mining. Our experimental results demonstrate that combining explicit category-level constraints with neural architectures effectively filters out co-purchase noise to surface recommendations that satisfy real-world user needs. Serving over 20 million active users monthly, the framework delivers significant uplifts in attributed GMV for organic discovery and drives substantial revenue growth in sponsored placements.

</details>


### [186] [Embedding Surgery: Localized Updates for Adaptive Ranking Correction in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05110)
*Maddalena Amendola,Antonio Mallia,Raffaele Perego*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"embedding surgery"（嵌入手术），一种轻量级的自适应排序校正方法。它通过在查询时对选中的文档嵌入进行局部最小化更新（由编辑反馈、用户交互或LLM伪标签引导），实现稠密检索的实时排序校正。该方法被形式化为凸优化问题，在保持全局嵌入结构的同时强制排序约束，在多个TREC基准和MS MARCO上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索系统是搜索引擎、推荐平台和RAG管道的核心组件，但其文档嵌入是离线计算并存储在静态索引中的，导致系统难以适应在线用户反馈或不断演化的搜索意图，缺乏实时调整排序结果的能力。

Method: 提出embedding surgery方法：在查询时对选定的文档嵌入施加局部最小更新，更新由编辑反馈、用户交互或大语言模型伪标签引导。将排序校正形式化为一个凸优化问题，在嵌入空间中强制执行排序约束的同时最小化对文档表征的改动。该方法可直接集成到标准稠密检索管道中。

Result: 在TREC DL、TREC Robust、TREC CAsT和MS MARCO基准上一致改进。在DL-Hard上通过编辑反馈实现nDCG@10最高+60.64%的相对提升，且在噪声或漂移反馈下依然稳健。排序校正可传播到语义相关的查询，嵌入更新可通过简单的原地覆写安全高效地应用于可扩展的ANN索引，且与CoRocchio等查询适配方法互补。

Conclusion: Embedding surgery是一种轻量、高效且可扩展的排序校正方案，能在不破坏嵌入空间全局结构、无需重建索引的前提下让稠密检索系统适应动态反馈。它与现有查询侧适配方法正交互补，并能抵御噪声反馈的干扰。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems are core components of modern search engines, recommendation platforms, and retrieval-augmented generation pipelines. They encode documents and queries into dense embeddings, enabling efficient semantic search via vector similarity. However, because document embeddings are computed offline and stored in static indexes, these systems struggle to adapt to user feedback or evolving search intent. To address this limitation, we introduce \emph{embedding surgery}, a lightweight approach for adaptive ranking correction in dense retrieval. The method applies localized, minimal updates to selected document embeddings at query time, guided by editorial feedback, user interactions, or pseudo-labels from large language models. We formulate embedding surgery as a convex optimization problem that enforces ranking constraints while minimizing modifications to the affected document representations. We integrate embedding surgery into standard dense retrieval pipelines and evaluate it on TREC Deep Learning, TREC Robust, TREC CAsT, and MS MARCO benchmarks. Results show consistent improvements (e.g., up to +60.64\% relative improvement in nDCG@10 on DL-Hard under editorial feedback), even under noisy or shifting feedback, with low computational cost and without disrupting the global structure of the embedding space. Extensive experiments show that ranking corrections propagate to semantically related queries and that embedding updates can be applied safely and efficiently to scalable Approximate Nearest Neighbor indexes via simple in-place overwriting, without requiring costly index reconstruction. Finally, embedding surgery complements query adaptation methods such as CoRocchio, yielding additional gains while being more robust to noisy feedback.

</details>
